FILE: doc/auth/change-password.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  CHANGE PASSWORD API — POST /v1/auth/change-password
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
================================================================


--------------------------------------------------
1. NO ACCESS TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "NewSecret456" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. VALIDATION ERRORS (Zod)
--------------------------------------------------

--- Missing oldPassword ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "newPassword": "NewSecret456" }'

Expected: 422


--- Missing newPassword ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 422


--- newPassword too short ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "Ab1" }'

Expected: 422


--- newPassword missing uppercase ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "newsecret123" }'

Expected: 422


--- newPassword missing lowercase ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "NEWSECRET123" }'

Expected: 422


--- newPassword missing number ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "NewSecretPass" }'

Expected: 422


--- Empty body ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{}'

Expected: 422


--- newPassword fails all conditions (no uppercase, no number) ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "abcdefgh" }'

Expected: 422


--------------------------------------------------
3. WRONG CURRENT PASSWORD (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "WrongPass1", "newPassword": "NewSecret456" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Current password is incorrect" }


--------------------------------------------------
4. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
for i in {1..6}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -b cookies.txt \
    -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "NewSecret456" }'
done

Expected: first 5 return 200, 6th returns 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. SUCCESSFUL CHANGE PASSWORD (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -v -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/change-password \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "oldPassword": "Secret123", "newPassword": "NewSecret456" }'

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Password changed successfully", "data": null }

Cookies cleared:
  Set-Cookie: accessToken=; Path=/; Max-Age=0
  Set-Cookie: refreshToken=; Path=/v1/auth; Max-Age=0

DB: password updated, all refresh_tokens revoked


--------------------------------------------------
6. OLD TOKENS REVOKED AFTER CHANGE
--------------------------------------------------
-- After changing password, try old refresh token:
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
  -b cookies-with-old-refresh.txt

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Refresh token revoked" }


============================================================
FILE: doc/auth/login.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  LOGIN API — POST /v1/auth/login
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
================================================================


--------------------------------------------------
1. VALIDATION ERRORS (Zod)
--------------------------------------------------

--- Missing email ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "password": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 422
{"success":false,"message":"Validation failed"}

--- Missing password ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "patrik@jadara.com" }'

Expected: 422
{"success":false,"message":"Validation failed"}

--- Invalid email format ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "notanemail", "password": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 422
{"success":false,"message":"Validation failed"}

--- Empty body ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{}'

Expected: 422


--- Empty password string ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "patrik@jadara.com", "password": "" }'

Expected: 422

--------------------------------------------------
2. EMAIL NOT FOUND (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "nobody@jadara.com", "password": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid email or password" }
DB: login_history NOT created (no user_id to reference)


--------------------------------------------------
3. WRONG PASSWORD (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "patrik@jadara.com", "password": "WrongPass1" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid email or password" }
DB: login_history inserted (user_id, ip, user_agent, success=false)


--------------------------------------------------
4. INACTIVE ACCOUNT (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- First set user status to inactive in DB:
-- UPDATE users SET status = 'inactive' WHERE id = 1;

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "patrik@jadara.com", "password": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Account is not active" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. INACTIVE USER + CORRECT PASSWORD (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Verify: status check happens BEFORE password check
-- UPDATE users SET status = 'inactive' WHERE id = 1;

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "patrik@jadara.com", "password": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Account is not active" }


--------------------------------------------------
6. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Send 11+ requests within 60 seconds:

for i in {1..11}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -d '{ "email": "patrik@jadara.com", "password": "WrongPass1" }'
done

Expected: first 10 return 401, 11th returns 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }
Headers: X-RateLimit-Limit: 10
         X-RateLimit-Remaining: 0
         Retry-After: 59


--------------------------------------------------
7. SUCCESSFUL LOGIN (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -v -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "patrik@jadara.com", "password": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 200
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Login successful",
  "data": {
    "user": {
      "id": 2,
      "email": "patrik@jadara.com",
      "status": "active",
      "profile": {
        "id": 2,
        "user_id": 2,
        "first_name": "Patrik",
        "last_name": "Jadara",
        "phone": "+213558340669",
        "date_of_birth": null,
        "gender": "Male",
        "avatar": null,
        "created_at": "2026-08-20T19:19:31.482Z",
        "updated_at": "2026-08-20T19:19:31.482Z"
      },
      "role": "Beneficiary"
    }
  }
}

Cookies set:
  Set-Cookie: accessToken=...; Path=/; HttpOnly; ...
  Set-Cookie: refreshToken=...; Path=/v1/auth; HttpOnly; ...

DB: refresh_tokens inserted (hashed, expires 7d)
    login_history inserted (success=true)

FILE: doc/auth/logout.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  LOGOUT API — POST /v1/auth/logout
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
================================================================


--------------------------------------------------
1. NO ACCESS TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. EXPIRED ACCESS TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer eyeyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.expired.token"

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Access token expired" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. INVALID ACCESS TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer invalid.token.value"

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid token" }


--------------------------------------------------
4. INACTIVE USER TRIES LOGOUT (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- First set user to inactive, then try logout:
-- UPDATE users SET status = 'inactive' WHERE id = 1;

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout \
  -b cookies.txt

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Account is not active" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
for i in {1..21}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout \
    -b cookies.txt
done

Expected: first 20 return 200, 21st returns 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }


--------------------------------------------------
6. SUCCESSFUL LOGOUT (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -v -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout \
  -b cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Logged out successfully", "data": null }

Cookies cleared:
  Set-Cookie: accessToken=; Path=/; Max-Age=0
  Set-Cookie: refreshToken=; Path=/v1/auth; Max-Age=0

DB: all refresh_tokens for user revoked


--------------------------------------------------
7. LOGOUT TWICE (still 200)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Second logout with same (now cleared) cookie:
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout \
  -b cookies-cleared.txt

Expected: 200 (idempotent)
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Logged out successfully", "data": null }


--------------------------------------------------
8. LOGOUT VIA BEARER HEADER (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -v -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/logout \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9..."

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Logged out successfully", "data": null }

Cookies cleared:
  Set-Cookie: accessToken=; Path=/; Max-Age=0
  Set-Cookie: refreshToken=; Path=/v1/auth; Max-Age=0

DB: all refresh_tokens for user revoked


============================================================
FILE: doc/auth/refresh-token.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  REFRESH TOKEN API — POST /v1/auth/refresh-token
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
================================================================


--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Refresh token required" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. EXPIRED TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "refreshToken": "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.expired.token" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Refresh token expired" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. INVALID TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "refreshToken": "invalid.token.value" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid refresh token" }


--------------------------------------------------
4. REVOKED TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
-- After logout, try the old refreshToken cookie:
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
  -b cookies-with-revoked-token.txt

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Refresh token revoked" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. INACTIVE USER TRIES REFRESH (401)
--------------------------------------------------
-- First set user to inactive, then try refresh with valid cookie:
-- UPDATE users SET status = 'inactive' WHERE id = 1;

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
  -b cookies.txt

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid refresh token" }


--------------------------------------------------
6. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
for i in {1..21}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
    -b cookies.txt
done

Expected: first 20 return 200, 21st returns 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }


--------------------------------------------------
6. SUCCESSFUL REFRESH (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -v -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
  -b cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Token refreshed", "data": null }

New cookies set:
  Set-Cookie: accessToken=...; Path=/; HttpOnly; ...
  Set-Cookie: refreshToken=...; Path=/v1/auth; HttpOnly; ...

DB: old refresh_tokens revoked, new one inserted


--------------------------------------------------
8. REFRESH VIA BODY (200)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Send refresh token in body instead of cookie:
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/refresh-token \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "refreshToken": "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9..." }'

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Token refreshed", "data": null }

New cookies set:
  Set-Cookie: accessToken=...; Path=/; HttpOnly; ...
  Set-Cookie: refreshToken=...; Path=/v1/auth; HttpOnly; ...

DB: old refresh_tokens revoked, new one inserted


============================================================
FILE: doc/auth/register.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  REGISTER API — POST /v1/auth/register
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
================================================================

Register creates a user depending on the role:

  - Beneficiary (role_id=1): JSON body -> active immediately (201) + cookies
  - Reviewer    (role_id=2): multipart files[] + domain_ids -> pending (201), NO cookies
  - Company     (role_id=3): multipart files[] -> pending (201), NO cookies
  - Admin (role_id=4): not allowed (400)

Rate limit: 30 requests per hour per IP (429 after.)


--------------------------------------------------
1. BENEFICIARY — SUCCESSFUL REGISTRATION (201)
--------------------------------------------------
Active immediately, cookies set.

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "email": "newuser@jadara.com",
    "password": "Secret123",
    "first_name": "Patrik",
    "last_name": "Jadara",
    "phone": "+213558340669",
    "gender": "Male",
    "role_id": 1
  }'

Expected: 201
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Registration successful",
  "data": {
    "user": {
      "id": 3,
      "email": "newuser@jadara.com",
      "status": "active",
      "profile": {
        "id": 3,
        "user_id": 3,
        "first_name": "Patrik",
        "last_name": "Jadara",
        "phone": "+213558340669",
        "date_of_birth": null,
        "gender": "Male",
        "avatar": null,
        "created_at": "2026-08-28T16:00:00.000Z",
        "updated_at": "2026-08-28T16:00:00.000Z"
      },
      "role": "Beneficiary"
    },
    "demand": null
  }

Cookies set:
  Set-Cookie: accessToken=...; Path=/; HttpOnly; ...
  Set-Cookie: refreshToken=...; Path=/v1/auth; HttpOnly; ...

DB: users (status=active) + profiles + refresh_tokens inserted


--------------------------------------------------
2. REVIEWER — SUCCESSFUL REGISTRATION (201, PENDING)
--------------------------------------------------
Multipart. Requires at least one domain (domain_ids) and at least
one proof document (files). User is created with status=pending and
a registration demand is created. NO cookies are set.

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -F "email=reviewer@jadara.com" \
  -F "password=Secret123" \
  -F "first_name=Review" \
  -F "last_name=Er" \
  -F "role_id=2" \
  -F "domain_ids=1" \
  -F "domain_ids=8" \
  -F "files=@/path/to/cv.pdf"

Expected: 201
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Registration submitted for review",
  "data": {
    "user": {
      "id": 5,
      "email": "reviewer@jadara.com",
      "status": "pending",
      "profile": {
        "id": 5,
        "user_id": 5,
        "first_name": "Review",
        "last_name": "Er",
        "phone": null,
        "date_of_birth": null,
        "gender": null,
        "avatar": null,
        "created_at": "...",
        "updated_at": "..."
      },
      "role": "Reviewer"
    },
    "demand": { "id": 1 }
  }

NO cookies set (user cannot log in until approved).

DB: users (status=pending) + profiles + demands (pending) +
    demand_domains (1 per requested domain) +
    documents (type=demand, uploaded to MinIO bucket "documents"
    under folder "demands/<userId>/")

domain_ids also works comma-separated: -F "domain_ids=1,8"


--------------------------------------------------
3. COMPANY — SUCCESSFUL REGISTRATION (201, PENDING)
--------------------------------------------------
Multipart. Requires at least one proof document (files). No domains.
User created with status=pending + demand. NO cookies.

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -F "email=company@jadara.com" \
  -F "password=Secret123" \
  -F "first_name=ACME" \
  -F "last_name=Corp" \
  -F "role_id=3" \
  -F "files=@/path/to/license.pdf"

Expected: 201
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Registration submitted for review",
  "data": {
    "user": {
      "id": 6,
      "email": "company@jadara.com",
      "status": "pending",
      "profile": {
        "id": 6,
        "user_id": 6,
        "first_name": "ACME",
        "last_name": "Corp",
        "phone": null,
        "date_of_birth": null,
        "gender": null,
        "avatar": null,
        "created_at": "...",
        "updated_at": "..."
      },
      "role": "Company"
    },
    "demand": { "id": 2 }
  }

NO cookies set.


--------------------------------------------------
4. VALIDATION ERRORS (Zod -> 422)
--------------------------------------------------
Happens for any role (Base Zod schema).

--- Missing email ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "password": "Secret123", "first_name": "P", "last_name": "J", "role_id": 1 }'

Expected: 422 {"success":false,"message":"Validation failed"}

--- Invalid email format ---
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "email": "notanemail", "password": "Secret123",
    "first_name": "P", "last_name": "J", "role_id": 1
  }'

Expected: 422

--- Password too short (< 8 chars) ---
... "password": "Ab1" ... -> 422

--- Password missing uppercase/lowercase/number ---
"password": "secret123" -> 422 (regex "Must contain uppercase, lowercase, and number")

--- Missing first_name / last_name --- -> 422

--- role_id missing --- -> 422

--- role_id negative --- -F "role_id=-1" -> 422

--- role_id invalid (e.g. 999) ---
Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Invalid role"}

--- Admin role not allowed ---
... "role_id": 4 ...
Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Invalid role"}


--------------------------------------------------
5. EMAIL ALREADY EXISTS (409)
--------------------------------------------------
Email uniqueness is global (users table), checked before role logic.

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "email": "patrik@jadara.com", "password": "Secret123",
    "first_name": "P", "last_name": "J", "role_id": 1
  }'

Expected: 409 {"success":false,"message":"Email already registered"}


--------------------------------------------------
6. REVIEWER WITHOUT DOMAIN (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -F "email=reviewer2@jadara.com" \
  -F "password=Secret123" -F "first_name=R" -F "last_name=E" \
  -F "role_id=2" \
  -F "files=@/path/to/cv.pdf"

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Reviewer must select at least one domain"}


--------------------------------------------------
7. NO DOCUMENT UPLOADED (400) — Reviewer or Company
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -F "email=company2@jadara.com" \
  -F "password=Secret123" -F "first_name=ACME" -F "last_name=Corp" \
  -F "role_id=3"

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"At least one document is required"}


--------------------------------------------------
8. INVALID DOMAIN IDS (400) — Reviewer
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
  -F "email=reviewer3@jadara.com" \
  -F "password=Secret123" -F "first_name=R" -F "last_name=E" \
  -F "role_id=2" -F "domain_ids=1" -F "domain_ids=999" \
  -F "files=@/path/to/cv.pdf"

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Some domains do not exist"}


--------------------------------------------------
9. UPLOAD LIMITS
--------------------------------------------------
- Up to 5 files (middleware upload.array("files", 5))
- Allowed MIME types: pdf, images, word, ppt, zip, txt (see
  framework/middleware/upload.ts)
- File size limited by env.UPLOAD_MAX_FILE_SIZE


--------------------------------------------------
10. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Send 31+ requests within 1 hour:
for i in $(seq 1 31); do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/register \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -d "{
      \"email\": \"user${i}@jadara.com\", \"password\": \"Secret123\",
      \"first_name\": \"User\", \"last_name\": \"${i}\", \"role_id\": 1
    }"
done

Expected: first 30 return 201, 31st returns 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }


--------------------------------------------------
11. LOGIN WHILE PENDING / NOT ACTIVE (403)
--------------------------------------------------
Pending or rejected users cannot log in:

curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "reviewer@jadara.com", "password": "Secret123" }'

Expected: 403 {"success":false,"message":"Account is not active"}

Same for any protected route (verifyAccessToken returns 403).

Normal login works only after admin approves the demand (see
doc/demands/approve-demand.txt).


--------------------------------------------------
12. SUMMARY — ROLE BEHAVIOR
--------------------------------------------------
Role           Body type            Status on create  Cookies   Demand row
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
Beneficiary    JSON (application/json)   active       yes       none
Reviewer       multipart files+domains   pending      no        yes
Company        multipart files           pending      no        yes

% ============================================================
% SECTION 1: INSTALLATION
% ============================================================
\section{Installation}

\subsection{Install PostgreSQL}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo apt update
sudo apt install postgresql postgresql-client
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Verify Installation}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
psql --version
\end{lstlisting}

Expected output:
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
psql (PostgreSQL) 16.14 (Ubuntu 16.14-0ubuntu0.24.04.1)
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Check Installed Packages}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
dpkg -l | grep postgresql
\end{lstlisting}

Key packages:
\begin{itemize}[noitemsep]
    \item \texttt{postgresql} -- Main server package
    \item \texttt{postgresql-16} -- Server binaries
    \item \texttt{postgresql-client-16} -- Client tools (psql)
    \item \texttt{postgresql-common} -- Cluster manager
\end{itemize}

% ============================================================
% SECTION 2: SERVICE MANAGEMENT
% ============================================================
\section{Service Management}

PostgreSQL runs as a systemd service on Ubuntu.

\subsection{Start / Stop / Restart}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo systemctl start postgresql
sudo systemctl stop postgresql
sudo systemctl restart postgresql
sudo systemctl reload postgresql
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Check Status}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo systemctl status postgresql
\end{lstlisting}

Expected output (running):
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
* postgresql.service - PostgreSQL RDBMS
     Loaded: loaded
     Active: active (exited) since ...
   Main PID: ...
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Enable on Boot}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo systemctl enable postgresql
sudo systemctl disable postgresql
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Check Listening Port}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo ss -tlnp | grep 5432
\end{lstlisting}

% ============================================================
% SECTION 3: CONFIGURATION
% ============================================================
\section{Configuration}

\subsection{Configuration File Locations}

\begin{table}[h]
\centering
\begin{tabular}{@{}ll@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{File} & \textbf{Purpose} \\
\midrule
\texttt{/etc/postgresql/16/main/postgresql.conf} & Main server config \\
\texttt{/etc/postgresql/16/main/pg\_hba.conf} & Client authentication \\
\texttt{/etc/postgresql/16/main/pg\_ident.conf} & User name mapping \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\caption{PostgreSQL configuration files}
\end{table}

\subsection{Key Settings in postgresql.conf}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo nano /etc/postgresql/16/main/postgresql.conf
\end{lstlisting}

Common settings:
\begin{lstlisting}
listen_addresses = 'localhost'
port = 5432
max_connections = 100
shared_buffers = '256MB'
work_mem = '4MB'
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Authentication (pg\_hba.conf)}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo nano /etc/postgresql/16/main/pg_hba.conf
\end{lstlisting}

Local connection (default):
\begin{lstlisting}
TYPE  DATABASE    USER        ADDRESS         METHOD
local   all         postgres                    peer
local   all         all                         scram-sha-256
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{notebox}
After changing \texttt{pg\_hba.conf}, reload PostgreSQL:
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo systemctl reload postgresql
\end{lstlisting}
\end{notebox}

% ============================================================
% SECTION 4: DATABASE \& USER CREATION
% ============================================================
\section{Database \& User Creation}

\subsection{Step 1: Connect as PostgreSQL Superuser}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo -u postgres psql
\end{lstlisting}

This opens the PostgreSQL prompt:

\subsection{Step 2: Create a New User}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql]
CREATE USER jadara_user WITH PASSWORD 'your_password_here';
\end{lstlisting}

Replace \texttt{your\_password\_here} with your actual password.

\begin{tipbox}
To generate a random 64-character password in the terminal (outside psql):
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
openssl rand -hex 32
\end{lstlisting}
This produces a 64-character hexadecimal string.
\end{tipbox}

\subsection{Step 3: Create the Database}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql]
CREATE DATABASE jadara;
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Step 4: Grant Privileges}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql]
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE jadara TO jadara_user;
ALTER USER jadara_user CREATEDB;
\end{lstlisting}

Connect to the jadara database and grant schema permissions:
\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql]
\c jadara
GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO jadara_user;
GRANT ALL ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO jadara_user;
GRANT ALL ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO jadara_user;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL ON TABLES TO jadara_user;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL ON SEQUENCES TO jadara_user;
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{notebox}
The \texttt{ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES} commands ensure that tables and sequences created by the \texttt{postgres} superuser during \texttt{prisma migrate} are automatically accessible to \texttt{jadara\_user}. Without these, Prisma migrations may fail with permission errors on the shadow database.
\end{notebox}

\subsection{Step 5: Verify Setup}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql]
SELECT current_user;
\q
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Step 6: Test Connection}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
psql -U jadara_user -d jadara -h localhost
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Complete SQL Script}

Here is the full script to run inside \texttt{psql} as the \texttt{postgres} user:

\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql]
CREATE USER jadara_user WITH PASSWORD 'your_password_here';
CREATE DATABASE jadara;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE jadara TO jadara_user;
ALTER USER jadara_user CREATEDB;
\c jadara
GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO jadara_user;
GRANT ALL ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO jadara_user;
GRANT ALL ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO jadara_user;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL ON TABLES TO jadara_user;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL ON SEQUENCES TO jadara_user;
\end{lstlisting}

% ============================================================
% SECTION 5: BACKUP & RESTORE
% ============================================================
\section{Backup \& Restore}

\subsection{Backup a Single Database}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
pg_dump -U jadara_user -h localhost jadara > jadara_backup.sql
pg_dump -U jadara_user -h localhost -Fc jadara > jadara_backup.dump
sudo -u postgres pg_dump jadara > jadara_backup.sql
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Backup All Databases}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
sudo -u postgres pg_dumpall > full_backup.sql
sudo -u postgres pg_dumpall --globals-only > globals.sql
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Restore a Database}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
psql -U jadara_user -d jadara -h localhost -f jadara_backup.sql
pg_restore -U jadara_user -d jadara -h localhost jadara_backup.dump
sudo -u postgres psql -f full_backup.sql
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Scheduled Backup with Cron}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell]
crontab -e
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{warningbox}
Always test your backups by restoring them to a test database!
\end{warningbox}

% ============================================================
% SECTION 6: USEFUL COMMANDS
% ============================================================
\section{Useful Commands}

\subsection{psql Meta-Commands}

\begin{table}[h]
\centering
\begin{tabular}{@{}ll@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Command} & \textbf{Description} \\
\midrule
\texttt{\textbackslash l} & List all databases \\
\texttt{\textbackslash c dbname} & Connect to database \\
\texttt{\textbackslash c} & Show current connection \\
\texttt{\textbackslash du} & List all users/roles \\
\texttt{\textbackslash dt} & List tables in schema \\
\texttt{\textbackslash ds} & List sequences \\
\texttt{\textbackslash di} & List indexes \\
\texttt{\textbackslash dv} & List views \\
\texttt{\textbackslash dn} & List schemas \\
\texttt{\textbackslash df} & List functions \\
\texttt{\textbackslash d tablename} & Describe table structure \\
\texttt{\textbackslash dp} & List table privileges \\
\texttt{\textbackslash x} & Toggle expanded output \\
\texttt{\textbackslash timing} & Toggle query timing \\
\texttt{\textbackslash i filename} & Execute SQL from file \\
\texttt{\textbackslash o filename} & Pipe output to file \\
\texttt{\textbackslash q} & Quit psql \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\caption{psql meta-commands}
\end{table}

\subsection{Useful SQL Queries}

\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql]
SELECT current_database();
SELECT current_user;
SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public';
SELECT pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size('table_name'));
SELECT pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size('jadara'));
SELECT * FROM pg_stat_activity;
SELECT pid, now() - pg_stat_activity.query_start AS duration, query, state
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state != 'idle'
ORDER BY duration DESC;
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{Connection Information}

\begin{table}[h]
\centering
\begin{tabular}{@{}ll@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Parameter} & \textbf{Value} \\
\midrule
Host & \texttt{localhost} (or \texttt{127.0.0.1}) \\
Port & \texttt{5432} \\
Database & \texttt{jadara} \\
User & \texttt{jadara\_user} \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\caption{Connection parameters}
\end{table}

\subsection{Connection String Format}

\begin{lstlisting}
postgresql://user:password@host:port/database
\end{lstlisting}

% ============================================================
% SECTION 7: QUICK REFERENCE CARD
% ============================================================
\newpage
\section{Quick Reference Card}

\begin{multicols}{2}

\subsection*{Installation}
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell,numbers=none]
sudo apt install postgresql
psql --version
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Service}
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell,numbers=none]
sudo systemctl start postgresql
sudo systemctl stop postgresql
sudo systemctl status postgresql
sudo systemctl restart postgresql
\end{lstlisting}

\columnbreak

\subsection*{Connect}
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell,numbers=none]
sudo -u postgres psql
psql -U user -d db -h localhost
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{User \& DB}
\begin{lstlisting}[style=sql,numbers=none]
CREATE USER name WITH PASSWORD 'pass';
CREATE DATABASE dbname;
GRANT ALL ON DATABASE dbname TO name;
ALTER USER name CREATEDB;
\c dbname
GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO name;
GRANT ALL ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO name;
GRANT ALL ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO name;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public
  GRANT ALL ON TABLES TO name;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public
  GRANT ALL ON SEQUENCES TO name;
\end{lstlisting}

\end{multicols}

\vspace{1em}

\begin{multicols}{2}

\subsection*{psql Commands}
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell,numbers=none]
\l          -- list databases
\du         -- list users
\c dbname   -- connect to db
\dt         -- list tables
\d tablename -- describe table
\q          -- quit
\end{lstlisting}

\columnbreak

\subsection*{Backup}
\begin{lstlisting}[style=shell,numbers=none]
pg_dump db > backup.sql
pg_dump -Fc db > backup.dump
pg_dumpall > full_backup.sql
psql -f backup.sql db
pg_restore -d db backup.dump
\end{lstlisting}

\end{multicols}

\vspace{1em}

\begin{notebox}
\textbf{Password placeholder:} Throughout this guide, \texttt{your\_password\_here} represents the actual password for \texttt{jadara\_user}. Generate one with: \texttt{openssl rand -hex 32}
\end{notebox}

\end{document}


============================================================
FILE: doc/demands/approve-demand.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  APPROVE DEMAND API — POST /v1/demands/:id/approve   (ACCEPT)
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Admin only (permission: manage_users)
  Rate limit: 20 requests per minute
================================================================

Accepts a pending registration demand:

1. user.status            -> "active"   (applicant can now log in)
2. Reviewer only:         -> reviewer_domains created for requested
                            domains (skipDuplicates)
3. demand.status          -> "approved"
4. demand.reviewed_by     -> admin user id
5. demand.review_note     -> note (optional)
6. demand.reviewed_at     -> now

Body (JSON, all optional): { "note": "..." }   (max 1000 chars)


--------------------------------------------------
1. APPROVE A REVIEWER
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/1/approve \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"note":"Documents verified"}' | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Demand approved",
  "data": {
    "id": 1,
    "status": "approved",
    "role": { "id": 2, "name": "Reviewer" },
    "applicant": {
      "id": 5,
      "email": "reviewer@jadara.com",
      "status": "active",
      "profiles": { "first_name": "Review", "last_name": "Er", "phone": null, "avatar": null }
    },
    "domains": [
      { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" },
      { "id": 8, "name": "Databases" }
    ],
    "documents": [ { "id": 3, "name": "cv.pdf", "type": "demand", "download_url": "...", ... } ],
    "review_note": "Documents verified",
    "reviewed_at": "2026-08-28T16:00:00.000Z",
    "created_at": "...",
    "updated_at": "..."
  }
}

DB effects:
  users.status = 'active'
  reviewer_domains: +1 row per requested domain
  demands.status = 'approved', reviewed_by=1, review_note set, reviewed_at set
  (all in one transaction)


--------------------------------------------------
2. APPROVE A COMPANY
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/2/approve \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{}'

Expected: 200
  demand status -> approved, user active.
  No reviewer_domains created (role is Company).


--------------------------------------------------
3. WITHOUT NOTE
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/1/approve \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{}'

review_note -> null


--------------------------------------------------
4. ALREADY REVIEWED (409)
--------------------------------------------------
Approving an already approved/rejected demand fails:

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/1/approve \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{}'

Expected: 409 {"success":false,"message":"Demand is already reviewed"}


--------------------------------------------------
5. DEMAND NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Demand not found"}


--------------------------------------------------
6. INVALID ID (400) / NO AUTH (401) / NOT ADMIN (403)
--------------------------------------------------
/demands/abc/approve        -> 400 Invalid demand ID
(no cookie)                 -> 401
ben_cookies                 -> 403 Forbidden


--------------------------------------------------
7. AFTER APPROVAL — APPLICANT LOGIN
--------------------------------------------------
The applicant can now log in normally with the same credentials:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"email":"reviewer@jadara.com","password":"Secret123"}'

Expected: 200 Login successful (user.status = "active")

Approved reviewers appear in GET /v1/domains/reviewers and
GET /v1/domains/:id/reviewers for their assigned domains.

============================================================
FILE: doc/demands/get-demand.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  GET DEMAND API — GET /v1/demands/:id
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Admin only (permission: manage_users)
  Rate limit: 60 requests per minute
================================================================

Returns one registration demand with full detail:
applicant profile, requested role, requested domains, proof
documents (each with a presigned download_url to view the PDF).


--------------------------------------------------
1. GET A DEMAND
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/1" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Demand fetched successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 1,
    "status": "pending",
    "role": { "id": 2, "name": "Reviewer" },
    "applicant": {
      "id": 5,
      "email": "reviewer@jadara.com",
      "status": "pending",
      "profiles": {
        "first_name": "Review",
        "last_name": "Er",
        "phone": null,
        "avatar": null
      }
    },
    "domains": [
      { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" },
      { "id": 8, "name": "Databases" }
    ],
    "documents": [
      {
        "id": 3,
        "name": "cv.pdf",
        "type": "demand",
        "file_url": "demands/5/uuid.pdf",
        "file_size": 12345,
        "mime_type": "application/pdf",
        "created_at": "...",
        "download_url": "http://localhost:9000/documents/demands/5/uuid.pdf?X-Amz-Algorithm=..."
      }
    ],
    "review_note": null,
    "reviewed_at": null,
    "created_at": "...",
    "updated_at": "..."
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
2. AFTER REVIEW (APPROVED/REJECTED)
--------------------------------------------------
The response adds review info:
  "status": "approved" | "rejected",
  "review_note": "Documents verified",
  "reviewed_at": "2026-08-28T16:00:00.000Z"
applicant.status is "active" (approved) or "inactive" (rejected).


--------------------------------------------------
3. VIEW / DOWNLOAD THE PDF
--------------------------------------------------
Copy the download_url from the response, then either:

Open in browser:
  xdg-open "http://localhost:9000/documents/demands/5/<uuid>.pdf?X-Amz-Algorithm=..."

Download to file:
  curl -s -o /tmp/cv.pdf "http://localhost:9000/documents/demands/5/<uuid>.pdf?X-Amz-Algorithm=..."

The presigned URL expires after 1 hour — fetch a fresh demand to get a
new one. Do not share the URL; it grants direct download access.


--------------------------------------------------
4. DEMAND NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/999"

Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Demand not found"}


--------------------------------------------------
5. INVALID ID (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/abc"

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Invalid demand ID"}


--------------------------------------------------
6. NOT ADMIN (403) / NO AUTH (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/1"   -> 403
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/1"                                    -> 401

============================================================
FILE: doc/demands/list-demands.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  LIST DEMANDS API — GET /v1/demands
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Admin only (permission: manage_users)
  Rate limit: 30 requests per minute
================================================================

Lists all registration demands (Reviewer / Company applications).

Query params:
  status  -> optional: pending | approved | rejected (filter)
  page    -> optional, default 1
  limit   -> optional, default 10, max 100

Documents include a download_url (MinIO presigned link, valid 1 hour).


--------------------------------------------------
1. LIST ALL DEMANDS
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Demands fetched successfully",
  "data": [
    {
      "id": 1,
      "status": "pending",
      "role": { "id": 2, "name": "Reviewer" },
      "applicant": {
        "id": 5,
        "email": "reviewer@jadara.com",
        "status": "pending",
        "profiles": {
          "first_name": "Review",
          "last_name": "Er",
          "phone": null,
          "avatar": null
        }
      },
      "domains": [
        { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" },
        { "id": 8, "name": "Databases" }
      ],
      "documents": [
        {
          "id": 3,
          "name": "cv.pdf",
          "type": "demand",
          "file_url": "demands/5/uuid.pdf",
          "file_size": 12345,
          "mime_type": "application/pdf",
          "created_at": "...",
          "download_url": "http://localhost:9000/documents/demands/5/uuid.pdf?X-Amz-Algorithm=..."
        }
      ],
      "review_note": null,
      "reviewed_at": null,
      "created_at": "...",
      "updated_at": "..."
    },
    { "id": 2, "status": "pending", "role": { "id": 3, "name": "Company" }, "applicant": { "email": "company@jadara.com", ... }, "domains": [], "documents": [...], ... }
  ],
  "meta": {
    "total": 2,
    "page": 1,
    "limit": 10,
    "totalPages": 1,
    "nextCursor": null
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
2. FILTER BY STATUS
--------------------------------------------------
Only pending:
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands?status=pending"

Only rejected:
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands?status=rejected"

Only approved:
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands?status=approved"

With pagination:
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands?page=1&limit=20"


--------------------------------------------------
3. INVALID STATUS (422)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands?status=foo"

Expected: 422 {"success":false,"message":"Validation failed"}


--------------------------------------------------
4. NOT ADMIN (403)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands"

Expected: 403 Forbidden (permission manage_users required)

--------------------------------------------------
5. NO AUTH (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/demands"

Expected: 401 Unauthorized (verifyAccessToken)


NOTES
- Response 'data' is the array; pagination is in 'meta'.
- applicant = user row + profiles (first_name/last_name/phone/avatar).
- domains = requested domains via demand_domains (empty for Company).
- documents = uploaded proof files + download_url to open/download the PDF.

============================================================
FILE: doc/demands/reject-demand.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  REJECT DEMAND API — POST /v1/demands/:id/reject   (REFUSE)
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Admin only (permission: manage_users)
  Rate limit: 20 requests per minute
================================================================

Refuses a pending registration demand:

1. user.status            -> "inactive"  (applicant can NOT log in)
2. demand.status          -> "rejected"
3. demand.reviewed_by     -> admin user id
4. demand.review_note     -> note (optional)
5. demand.reviewed_at     -> now

Body (JSON, all optional): { "note": "..." }   (max 1000 chars)


--------------------------------------------------
1. REJECT A DEMAND
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/2/reject \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"note":"Missing company license"}' | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Demand rejected",
  "data": {
    "id": 2,
    "status": "rejected",
    "role": { "id": 3, "name": "Company" },
    "applicant": {
      "id": 6,
      "email": "company@jadara.com",
      "status": "inactive",
      "profiles": { "first_name": "ACME", "last_name": "Corp", "phone": null, "avatar": null }
    },
    "domains": [],
    "documents": [ { "id": 4, "name": "license.pdf", "type": "demand", "download_url": "...", ... } ],
    "review_note": "Missing company license",
    "reviewed_at": "2026-08-28T16:00:00.000Z",
    "created_at": "...",
    "updated_at": "..."
  }
}

DB effects (one transaction):
  users.status = 'inactive'
  demands.status = 'rejected', reviewed_by=1, review_note set, reviewed_at set


--------------------------------------------------
2. WITHOUT NOTE
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/2/reject \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{}'

review_note -> null


--------------------------------------------------
3. ALREADY REVIEWED (409)
--------------------------------------------------
Rejecting an already approved/rejected demand fails:

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/demands/2/reject \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{}'

Expected: 409 {"success":false,"message":"Demand is already reviewed"}


--------------------------------------------------
4. DEMAND NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Demand not found"}


--------------------------------------------------
5. INVALID ID (400) / NO AUTH (401) / NOT ADMIN (403)
--------------------------------------------------
/demands/abc/reject        -> 400 Invalid demand ID
(no cookie)                -> 401
ben_cookies                -> 403 Forbidden


--------------------------------------------------
6. AFTER REJECTION — APPLICANT LOGIN BLOCKED
--------------------------------------------------
Login returns 403:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"email":"company@jadara.com","password":"Secret123"}'

Expected: 403 {"success":false,"message":"Account is not active"}

Protected routes also return 403 (verifyAccessToken).
The email stays reserved (409 on re-register with same email).

FILE: doc/diagrams/class_diag/cv_profile_class_diagram.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - CV & Profile Data Class Diagram
title CV & Profile Data - Class Diagram

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam nodeFontSize 11
skinparam classAttributeFontSize 10
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
hide empty members

' =========================================================
' ENUMS
' =========================================================

enum UserStatus {
    active
    inactive
    locked
    pending
}

enum LanguageProficiency {
    native
    fluent
    advanced
    intermediate
    beginner
}

enum SkillLevel {
    beginner
    intermediate
    advanced
    expert
}

' =========================================================
' CLASSES
' =========================================================

class users {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **email** : String (UNIQUE)
    + password : String
    + status : UserStatus
    + **role_id** : Int (FK)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
}

class work_experience {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + company : String
    + job_title : String
    + description : String
    + start_date : DateTime
    + end_date : DateTime
    + is_current : Boolean
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getWorkExperience(userId) : Object[]
    + createWorkExperience(userId, data) : Object
    + updateWorkExperience(userId, id, data) : Object
    + deleteWorkExperience(userId, id) : void
}

class education {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + school : String
    + degree : String
    + field_of_study : String
    + description : String
    + start_date : DateTime
    + end_date : DateTime
    + is_current : Boolean
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getEducation(userId) : Object[]
    + createEducation(userId, data) : Object
    + updateEducation(userId, id, data) : Object
    + deleteEducation(userId, id) : void
}

class certifications {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + name : String
    + issuer : String
    + issue_date : DateTime
    + expiry_date : DateTime
    + credential_url : String
    + file_url : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getCertifications(userId) : Object[]
    + createCertification(userId, data) : Object
    + updateCertification(userId, id, data) : Object
    + deleteCertification(userId, id) : void
}

class user_languages {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + **language_id** : Int (FK)
    + proficiency : LanguageProficiency
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getLanguages(userId) : Object[]
    + createLanguage(userId, data) : Object
    + updateLanguage(userId, id, data) : Object
    + deleteLanguage(userId, id) : void
}

class languages {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + code : String (UNIQUE)
    + status : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAll() : Language[]
    + findById(id) : Language
    + findByName(name) : Language
    + create(name) : Language
    + update(id, name) : Language
    + countUserLanguages(id) : number
    + remove(id) : void
}

class user_skills {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + **skill_id** : Int (FK)
    + level : SkillLevel
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getSkills(userId) : Object[]
    + findUserById(id) : User
    + findExistingSkillIds(ids) : number[]
    + findLinkedSkillIds(userId) : number[]
    + addSkills(userId, skillIds) : void
    + findLink(userId, skillId) : Object
    + updateLevel(userId, skillId, level) : void
    + removeLink(userId, skillId) : void
}

class skills {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + description : String
    + status : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAll() : Skill[]
    + findById(id) : Skill
    + findByName(name) : Skill
    + create(name) : Skill
    + update(id, name) : Skill
    + countUserSkills(id) : number
    + countDomainLinks(id) : number
    + remove(id) : void
}

class user_socials {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + platform : String
    + url : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + addSocial(userId, platform, url) : Object
    + removeSocial(userId, socialId) : void
}

' =========================================================
' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

users "1" -- "0..*" work_experience
users "1" -- "0..*" education
users "1" -- "0..*" certifications
users "1" -- "0..*" user_socials
users "1" -- "0..*" user_languages
users "1" -- "0..*" user_skills
languages "1" -- "0..*" user_languages
skills "1" -- "0..*" user_skills

users ..> UserStatus : uses
user_languages ..> LanguageProficiency : uses
user_skills ..> SkillLevel : uses

@enduml

FILE: doc/diagrams/class_diag/demands_class_diagram.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - Registration Demands Class Diagram
title Registration Demands - Class Diagram

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam nodeFontSize 11
skinparam classAttributeFontSize 10
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
hide empty members

' =========================================================
' ENUMS
' =========================================================

enum UserStatus {
    active
    inactive
    locked
    pending
}

enum DemandStatus {
    pending
    approved
    rejected
}

' =========================================================
' CLASSES
' =========================================================

class users {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **email** : String (UNIQUE)
    + password : String
    + status : UserStatus
    + **role_id** : Int (FK)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
}

class roles {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + is_selectable : Boolean
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
}

class domains {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + **created_by** : Int (FK)
    + status : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
}

class demands {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + **role_id** : Int (FK)
    + status : DemandStatus
    + **reviewed_by** : Int (FK)
    + review_note : String
    + reviewed_at : DateTime
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAll(page, limit, status?) : { demands, total }

title Users & Auth - Class Diagram

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam nodeFontSize 11
skinparam classAttributeFontSize 10
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
hide empty members

' =========================================================
' ENUM
' =========================================================

enum UserStatus {
    active
    inactive
    locked
    pending
}

' =========================================================
' CLASSES
' =========================================================

class users {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **email** : String (UNIQUE)
    + password : String
    + status : UserStatus
    + **role_id** : Int (FK)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getAll(query: { page?, limit? }) : Object
    + getById(id: number) : Object
    + update(id: number, data, adminUserId: number) : Object
    + remove(id: number, adminUserId: number) : void
    + getPublicProfile(id: number) : Object
    + getMyProfile(userId: number) : Object
    + updateMyProfile(userId: number, data, file?) : Object
    + getActivity(userId: number) : Object
    + addSocial(userId: number, platform: string, url: string) : Object
    + removeSocial(userId: number, socialId: number) : void
}

class profiles {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK, UNIQUE)
    + first_name : String
    + last_name : String
    + phone : String
    + date_of_birth : DateTime
    + gender : String
    + avatar : String
    + bio : String
    + location : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + update(id: number, data) : Object
    + updateAvatar(userId: number, avatarUrl: string) : Object
    + findProfileById(id: number) : Object
    + findPublicProfileById(id: number) : Object
}

class roles {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + is_selectable : Boolean
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAll() : Role[]
    + findById(id: number) : Role
    + create(name: string) : Role
    + update(id: number, name: string) : Role
    + delete(id: number) : void
    + countUsersByRoleId(id: number) : number
    + getUsersByRoleId(roleId: number) : Object[]
    + getPermissionsByRoleId(roleId: number) : Permission[]
}

class permissions {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAllPermissions() : Permission[]
    + findPermissionById(id: number) : Permission
    + createPermission(name: string) : Permission
    + updatePermission(id: number, name: string) : Permission
    + deletePermission(id: number) : void
    + countRolesByPermissionId(id: number) : number
    + getRolesByPermissionId(permissionId: number) : Role[]
}

class role_permissions {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **role_id** : Int (FK)
    + **permission_id** : Int (FK)
    + created_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getPermissionsByRoleId(roleId: number) : Permission[]
    + addPermissionToRole(roleId: number, permissionId: number) : void
    + removePermissionFromRole(roleId: number, permissionId: number) : void
    + isPermissionAssignedToRole(roleId: number, permissionId: number) : boolean
    + findRolesByPermissionId(permissionId: number) : Role[]
}

class refresh_tokens {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + **token** : String (UNIQUE)
    + expires_at : DateTime
    + created_at : DateTime
    ..
    + create(userId: number, rawToken: string, tx?) : RefreshToken
    + revokeAllByUserId(userId: number) : void
    + deleteExpired() : void
}

class login_history {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + ip_address : String
    + user_agent : String
    + login_at : DateTime
    + success : Boolean
    ..
    + create(userId: number, ipAddress: string, userAgent: string, success: boolean, tx?) : LoginHistory
    + findByUserId(userId: number, limit: number) : LoginHistory[]
}

class devices {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK)
    + **device_fingerprint** : String (UNIQUE)
    + device_name : String
    + last_active : DateTime
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + upsert(userId: number, ip: string, userAgent: string, tx?) : Device
}

' =========================================================
' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

roles "1" -- "0..*" users
users "1" -- "1" profiles
users "1" -- "0..*" refresh_tokens
users "1" -- "0..*" login_history
users "1" -- "0..*" devices
roles "1" -- "0..*" role_permissions
permissions "1" -- "0..*" role_permissions

users ..> UserStatus : uses

@enduml

@startuml
' JADARA - Audit Logs Class Diagram
title Audit Logs - Class Diagram

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam nodeFontSize 11
skinparam classAttributeFontSize 10
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
hide empty members

' =========================================================
' ENUMS
' =========================================================

enum AuditAction {
    CREATE
    UPDATE
    DELETE
}

' =========================================================
' CLASSES
' =========================================================

class audit_logs {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + user_id : Int
    + action : AuditAction
    + entity : String
    + entity_id : Int
    + old_values : Json
    + new_values : Json
    + ip_address : String
    + user_agent : String
    + created_at : DateTime
}

' =========================================================
' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

audit_logs ..> AuditAction : uses

note right of audit_logs
  No repository exists for audit_logs,
  so the class has no methods.

  It is also absent from the old
  class_diag set, which is why this
  diagram is new.

  user_id is a bare Int with no @relation
  to users, so it is not a real foreign
  key. Drawn with no edge on purpose.
end note

note on link
  action changed from String VarChar(100)
  to the AuditAction enum. Existing rows
  need a USING cast and a casing decision,
  since the old values were likely lowercase
  (create/update/delete) and will not match
  CREATE/UPDATE/DELETE.
end note

@enduml

note right of social_platforms
  NEW table. No repository exists yet,
  so it has no methods.
end note

note right of skill_categories
  NEW table. No repository exists yet,
  so it has no methods.
end note

note right of cv_versions
  NEW table. No repository exists yet,
  so it has no methods.

  last_audit_id is a required Int with
  no relation to audit_logs.
  Dangling reference, not enforced.
end note

note right of cv_generation_requests
  STALE: createRequest(userId, status, ...)
  does not pass language, which is now
  required. status also lost its default,
  so callers must always supply it.
end note

note on link
  skills.category_id is required and has no
  default, so every existing skill row needs
  a category backfill before this FK is added.
end note

note right of skills
  STALE: create() does not set the new
  required category_id.
end note

' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

roles "1" -- "0..*" users
users "1" -- "0..*" demands : applicant
users "0..1" -- "0..*" demands : reviewer
roles "1" -- "0..*" demands
demands "1" -- "0..*" demand_domains
domains "1" -- "0..*" demand_domains
demands "0..1" -- "0..*" documents
users "1" -- "0..*" documents
users "1" -- "0..*" reviewer_domains

users "1" -- "0..*" cv_generation_requests

users ..> UserStatus : uses
demands ..> DemandStatus : uses
cv_generation_requests ..> CVLanguage : uses

note right of reviewer_domains
  user_id is UNIQUE on its own, replacing
  @@unique([user_id, domain_id]).
  setReviewerDomains
  write one row per domain, so approving a
  reviewer with 2+ domains now fails.
end note

note right of demands
  approve() copies the picked demand domains
  into reviewer_domains, so it inherits that
  same one-domain-per-reviewer limit.
end note

note right of cv_generation_requests
  STALE: createRequest() does not pass the
  now-required language.
end note

note right of demand_domains
  createMany() is a Prisma client call, not a
  repository method. It is invoked from
  auth.service during registration, not from
  a demands repository.
end note

@enduml


============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/class_diag2/explain.txt
============================================================
Sure — in normal words:

One User can have many User Skills.
Example: Ahmed has Teamwork, Communication, etc.

One User can submit many Volunteer Applications.
Example: Ahmed applies to Beach Cleaning and Tree Planting.

One User can have many Completed Volunteering records.
Example: Ahmed completed 3 volunteering activities.

An Admin is a User who can approve volunteering activities.
One Admin can approve many activities.

One Skill can belong to many Users.
This is handled through the existing user_skills table.

One Skill can be required by many Activities.
For example, Teamwork can be required by Beach Cleaning and Tree Planting.

One User can have one Organization profile at most.
This represents a user who manages an organization account.

One Organization can create many Volunteering Activities.
Example: Green Algeria creates Beach Cleaning, Tree Planting, etc.

One Activity Category can contain many Activities.
Example: Environmental can contain Beach Cleaning and Tree Planting.

One Activity can require many Skills.
Example: Beach Cleaning requires Teamwork and Communication.

One Activity can receive many Volunteer Applications.
Example: 30 users can apply to the same activity.

One Completed Volunteering record belongs to one Activity.
One Organization can have many Completed Volunteering records, because many volunteers can complete activities belonging to that organization.

The most important chain is:

Organization → Activity → Applications → Users

and for skills:

User → User Skills → Skills ← Required Skills ← Activity

and after completion:

User → Completed Volunteering → Activity → Organization.

@startuml
' JADARA - Full Class Diagram (all entities)
title JADARA - Full Class Diagram

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam nodeFontSize 11
skinparam classAttributeFontSize 10
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
hide empty members

' =========================================================
' ENUMS
' =========================================================

enum UserStatus {
    active
    inactive
    locked
    pending
}

enum DemandStatus {
    pending
    approved
    rejected
}

enum ProjectStatus {
    draft
    submitted
    under_review
    verified
}

enum SkillLevel {
    beginner
    intermediate
    advanced
    expert
}

enum LanguageProficiency {
    native
    fluent
    advanced
    intermediate
    beginner
}

enum AuditAction {
    CREATE
    UPDATE
    DELETE
}

enum CVLanguage {
    AR
    EN
    FR
}

' =========================================================
' CLASSES
' =========================================================

class users {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **email** : String (UNIQUE)
    + password : String
    + status : UserStatus
    + **role_id** : Int (FK)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAll(page, limit) : Object
    + findById(id) : Object
    + findByEmail(email) : Object
    + findRoleById(id) : Object
    + update(id, data, adminUserId) : Object
    + delete(id, adminUserId) : void
    + updateAvatar(userId, avatarUrl) : void
    + findPublicProfileById(id) : Object
    + findProfileById(id) : Object
    + getActivity(userId) : Object
    + addSocial(userId, platform, url) : Object
    + removeSocial(userId, socialId) : void
}

class profiles {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK, UNIQUE)
    + first_name : String
    + last_name : String
    + phone : String
    + date_of_birth : DateTime
    + gender : String
    + avatar : String
    + bio : String
    + location : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findProfileById(id) : Object
    + findPublicProfileById(id) : Object
    + updateAvatar(userId, avatarUrl) : void
}

class roles {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + is_selectable : Boolean
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAll() : Role[]
    + findById(id) : Role
    + findByName(name) : Role
    + create(name) : Role
    + update(id, name) : Role
    + countUsersByRoleId(roleId) : number
    + getUsersByRoleId(roleId) : User[]
}

class permissions {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **name** : String (UNIQUE)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAllPermissions() : Permission[]
    + findPermissionById(id) : Permission
    + findPermissionByName(name) : Permission
    + createPermission(name) : Permission
    + updatePermission(id, name) : Permission
    + deletePermission(id) : void
    + countRolesByPermissionId(id) : number
    + getRolesByPermissionId(id) : Role[]

class role_permissions {

' =========================================================

roles "1" -- "0..*" users
users "1" -- "1" profiles

permissions "1" -- "0..*" role_permissions
users "1" -- "0..*" refresh_tokens
users "1" -- "0..*" login_history

users "1" -- "0..*" user_languages
languages "1" -- "0..*" user_languages
users "1" -- "0..*" user_skills
skills "1" -- "0..*" user_skills
skill_categories "1" -- "0..*" skills
skills "1" -- "0..*" skill_domains
domains "1" -- "0..*" skill_domains
domains "1" -- "0..*" demand_domains
users "1" -- "0..*" user_socials
social_platforms "1" -- "0..*" user_socials
domains "1" -- "0..*" sub_domains
sub_domains "0..1" -- "0..*" projects
users "1" -- "0..*" projects
projects "1" -- "0..*" evidence
projects "1" -- "0..*" project_reviews
users "1" -- "0..*" project_reviews : reviewer
users "1" -- "0..*" reviewer_domains
domains "1" -- "0..*" reviewer_domains
users "1" -- "0..*" demands : applicant
users "0..1" -- "0..*" demands : reviewer
roles "1" -- "0..*" demands
demands "1" -- "0..*" demand_domains
demands "0..1" -- "0..*" documents
users "1" -- "0..*" documents
users "1" -- "0..*" cv_generation_requests
users "1" -- "0..*" cv_versions

users ..> UserStatus : uses
demands ..> DemandStatus : uses
projects ..> ProjectStatus : uses
user_skills ..> SkillLevel : uses
user_languages ..> LanguageProficiency : uses
audit_logs ..> AuditAction : uses
cv_generation_requests ..> CVLanguage : uses
cv_versions ..> CVLanguage : uses

note right of reviewer_domains
  user_id UNIQUE alone replaced
  @@unique([user_id, domain_id]).
  STALE: setReviewerDomains() writes one row
  per domain, so a 2-domain call now
  violates this constraint.
end note

note right of projects
  STALE: findExistingDomain(id) and create()
  still use domain_id, now sub_domain_id.
  updateStatus() already matches the new
  ProjectStatus enum.
end note

note right of skills
  skill_domains[] was restored, so
  countDomainLinks() resolves again.
  create() still does not set the new
  required category_id.
end note

note right of user_socials
  STALE: addSocial() still takes a platform
  string, not a platform_id.
end note

note right of cv_generation_requests
  STALE: createRequest() does not pass the
  now-required language. status also lost
  its default.
end note

note right of sub_domains
  NEW table, no repository yet.

  AUTHORIZATION GAP
  reviewer_domains -> domains
  projects -> sub_domains
  No path from a reviewer's authorized
  domain to a project's domain, so
  findAvailable(reviewerId, domainIds)
  can no longer filter by reviewer scope.
end note

note right of skill_categories
  NEW table, no repository yet.
end note

note right of social_platforms
  NEW table, no repository yet.
end note

note right of cv_versions
  NEW table, no repository yet.
  last_audit_id has no @relation
  to audit_logs.
end note

note right of audit_logs
  No repository exists. user_id has no
  @relation to users, so no edge is drawn.
  action changed from String to the
  AuditAction enum, needing a USING cast.
end note

note right of demand_domains
  createMany() is a Prisma client call, not a
  repository method. It is invoked from
  auth.service during registration, not from
  a demands repository.
end note

@enduml

' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

domains "1" -- "0..*" sub_domains
sub_domains "0..1" -- "0..*" projects
skill_categories "1" -- "0..*" skills
skills "1" -- "0..*" user_skills

users ..> UserStatus : uses
projects ..> ProjectStatus : uses
user_skills ..> SkillLevel : uses

note right of sub_domains
  NEW table. No repository exists yet,
  so it has no methods.
end note

note right of skill_categories
  NEW table. No repository exists yet,
  so it has no methods.
end note

note right of reviewer_domains
  user_id is now UNIQUE on its own, replacing
  @@unique([user_id, domain_id]).
  setReviewerDomains writes one row per
  domainId, so a multi-domain call now
  violates this constraint.
end note

note right of projects
  STALE: findExistingDomain(id) and create()
  still use domain_id. The column is now
  sub_domain_id. updateStatus already
  matches the new ProjectStatus enum.
end note

note right of skills
  skill_domains[] was restored, so
  countDomainLinks(id) resolves again.
  create() still does not set the new
  required category_id.
end note

note bottom of sub_domains
  AUTHORIZATION GAP
  reviewer_domains.domain_id -> domains
  projects.sub_domain_id  -> sub_domains
  No edge links a reviewer's authorized
  domain to a project's domain any more.
  findAvailable(reviewerId, domainIds) can
  no longer filter projects by reviewer scope.
end note

' JADARA - Users & Auth Class Diagram

enum UserStatus {
    active
    inactive
    locked
    pending
}

' =========================================================
' CLASSES
' =========================================================

class users {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **email** : String (UNIQUE)
    + password : String
    + status : UserStatus
    + **role_id** : Int (FK)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + findAll(page, limit) : Object
    + findById(id) : Object
    + findByEmail(email) : Object
    + findRoleById(id) : Object
    + update(id, data, adminUserId) : Object
    + delete(id, adminUserId) : void
    + updateAvatar(userId, avatarUrl) : void
    + findPublicProfileById(id) : Object
    + findProfileById(id) : Object
    + getActivity(userId) : Object
}
