class companies {
    + **id** : Int (PK)
    + **user_id** : Int (FK, UNIQUE)
    + **slug** : String (UNIQUE)
    + **name** : String
    + legal_name : String
    + description : String
    + logo : String
    + website : String
    + email : String
    + phone : String
    + industry : String
    + size : String
    + location : String
    + country : String
    + status : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
}

' =========================================================
' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

roles "1" -- "0..*" users
users "1" -- "1" profiles
users "1" -- "0..*" refresh_tokens
users "1" -- "0..*" login_history
users "1" -- "0..*" devices
roles "1" -- "0..*" role_permissions
permissions "1" -- "0..*" role_permissions
users "0..1" -- "1" companies : company role only

users ..> UserStatus : uses

note right of companies
  PROPOSED. Not in prisma/schema.prisma yet,
  and no repository exists, so it has no
  methods.

  Used only by users whose role_id points to
  the company role. The 0..1 side means a
  company account has at most one company
  profile; a beneficiary or reviewer user
  has none.

  user_id is UNIQUE, so one company profile
  per user. name is indexed but not unique
  to allow shared or trading names;
  slug is the unique public identifier.
end note

@enduml

' =========================================================
' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

' -------------------------
' User
' -------------------------

users "1" -- "0..*" user_skills
users "1" -- "0..*" user_completed_volunteering

' Admin approval
users "0..1" -- "0..*" volunteering_activities : approves

' -------------------------
' Skills
' -------------------------

skills "1" -- "0..*" user_skills
skills "1" -- "0..*" activity_required_skills

' -------------------------
' Organization
' -------------------------

users "1" -- "0..1" organizations : organization account

organizations "1" -- "0..*" volunteering_activities : creates / owns

' -------------------------
' Activity Category
' -------------------------

activity_categories "1" -- "0..*" volunteering_activities

' -------------------------
' Activity Required Skills
' -------------------------

volunteering_activities "1" -- "0..*" activity_required_skills

' -------------------------
' Applications
' -------------------------

volunteering_activities "1" -- "0..*" volunteer_applications

users "1" -- "0..*" volunteer_applications : applies

' -------------------------
' Completed Volunteering
' -------------------------

volunteering_activities "1" -- "0..*" user_completed_volunteering

organizations "1" -- "0..*" user_completed_volunteering

' =========================================================
' DEPENDENCIES / ENUM USAGE
' =========================================================

volunteering_activities ..> ActivityStatus : uses
volunteer_applications ..> ApplicationStatus : uses

' =========================================================
' NOTES
' =========================================================

note right of users
  Existing JADARA entity.

  All users are centralized in
  the existing users table.

  Admins are users with the
  appropriate role/permissions.
end note

note right of skills
  Existing JADARA entity.

  Skills are shared across the
  platform and are NOT duplicated
  for the volunteering module.
end note

note right of user_skills
  Existing JADARA relationship.

  Reused by volunteering to
  calculate Skill Match.

  No new UserSkill table is
  created for volunteering.
end note

note right of activity_required_skills
  Junction table between an
  activity and its required skills.

  One activity can require
  multiple skills.

  One skill can be required
  by multiple activities.
end note

note right of volunteer_applications
  One user can apply only once
  to the same activity.

  Recommended database constraint:

  UNIQUE(user_id, activity_id)

  Skill Match helps the
  organization evaluate applicants.

  Accept / Reject remains a
  manual organization decision.
end note

note right of user_completed_volunteering
  Created only when a volunteer
  application reaches COMPLETED.

  Used to make querying completed
  volunteering experiences easier
  for the CV system.

  No audit system or CV versioning
  is included here.
end note

@enduml

    beginner

    beginner
    intermediate
    advanced
    expert

' =========================================================
' ENTITIES
' =========================================================

together {
  entity "users" as users {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **email : String (UNIQUE)**
    * password : String
    * status : UserStatus
    * **role_id : Int (FK)**
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "work_experience" as work_experience {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * company : String
    * job_title : String
    description : String
    start_date : DateTime
    end_date : DateTime
    is_current : Boolean
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "education" as education {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * school : String
    * degree : String
    field_of_study : String
    description : String
    start_date : DateTime
    end_date : DateTime
    is_current : Boolean
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "certifications" as certifications {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * name : String
    issuer : String
    issue_date : DateTime
    expiry_date : DateTime
    credential_url : String
    file_url : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "user_languages" as user_languages {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * **language_id : Int (FK)**
    * proficiency : LanguageProficiency
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
    -- (user_id, language_id) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "languages" as languages {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **name : String (UNIQUE)**
    code : String (UNIQUE)
    status : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "user_skills" as user_skills {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * **skill_id : Int (FK)**
    level : SkillLevel
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
    -- (user_id, skill_id) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "skills" as skills {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **name : String (UNIQUE)**
    description : String
    status : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "user_socials" as user_socials {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * platform : String
    * url : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
    -- (user_id, platform) (UNIQUE)
  }
}

legend right
  |= Symbol |= Meaning |
  | `||` | exactly one |
  | `o|` | zero or one |
  | `o{` | zero or many |

    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "user_skills" as user_skills {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * **skill_id : Int (FK)**
    level : SkillLevel
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
    -- (user_id, skill_id) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "skills" as skills {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **name : String (UNIQUE)**
    description : String
    status : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "user_socials" as user_socials {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * platform : String
    * url : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
    -- (user_id, platform) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "domains" as domains {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **name : String (UNIQUE)**
    description : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "skill_domains" as skill_domains {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **skill_id : Int (FK)**
    * **domain_id : Int (FK)**
    created_at : DateTime
    -- (skill_id, domain_id) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "reviewer_domains" as reviewer_domains {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * **domain_id : Int (FK)**
    created_at : DateTime
    -- (user_id, domain_id) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "projects" as projects {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * title : String
    description : String
    status : ProjectStatus
    github_url : String
    live_url : String
    figma_url : String
    **domain_id : Int (FK)**
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "evidence" as evidence {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **project_id : Int (FK)**
    * type : String
    * title : String
    description : String
    file_url : String
    external_url : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "project_reviews" as project_reviews {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **project_id : Int (FK)**
    * **reviewer_id : Int (FK)**
    * rating : Int
    feedback : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
    -- (project_id, reviewer_id) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "documents" as documents {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * name : String
    * type : String
    * file_url : String
    * file_size : Int
    * mime_type : String
    status : String
    **demand_id : Int (FK)**
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "demands" as demands {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * **role_id : Int (FK)**
    * status : DemandStatus
    **reviewed_by : Int (FK)**
    review_note : String
    reviewed_at : DateTime
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }

  entity "demand_domains" as demand_domains {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **demand_id : Int (FK)**
    * **domain_id : Int (FK)**
    created_at : DateTime
    -- (demand_id, domain_id) (UNIQUE)
  }

  entity "cv_generation_requests" as cv_generation_requests {
    * **id : Int (PK)**
    * **user_id : Int (FK)**
    * status : String
    missing_fields : Json
    data_hash : String
    file_url : String
    file_size : Int
    error_message : String
    created_at : DateTime
    updated_at : DateTime
  }
}

roles ||--o{ users : has
users ||--|| profiles : has
users ||--o{ refresh_tokens : owns
users ||--o{ login_history : owns
users ||--o{ devices : uses

users ||--o{ work_experience : has

users ||--o{ projects : owns

projects ||--o{ project_reviews : receives
users ||--o{ project_reviews : reviews

users ||--o{ documents : owns

  |= Symbol |= Meaning |
  | `||` | exactly one |
  | `o|` | zero or one |

FILE: doc/diagrams/sequence_diag/sequence_registration_beneficiary.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - Beneficiary Registration Sequence (no files)
title Beneficiary Registration - Sequence Diagram

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam actor {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam participant {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}

actor "Beneficiary" as client

participant "RateLimit\n+ zodValidate\n(middlewares)" as mid
participant "AuthController" as ctrl
participant "AuthService" as svc
participant "Hash\n(argon2)" as hash
participant "Prisma / DB" as db
participant "RefreshTokenRepo" as rtr
participant "JWT\n(jwtUtils)" as jwt

client -> mid : POST /v1/auth/register\n(email, password, first_name,\nlast_name, role_id)\n(no files)
alt rate limit / validation error
    mid --> client : 429 / 400
end

mid -> ctrl : authController.register
ctrl -> svc : register(data, files = [])

svc -> db : users.findUnique({ email })
alt email exists
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 409
    ctrl --> client : 409 "Email already registered"
end
alt role not selectable
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "Invalid role"
end

svc -> hash : hash.password(password)

svc -> db : $transaction - users.create\n(status=active) + profiles.create\n(first_name, last_name, phone?, gender?)
db --> svc : user (role: Beneficiary)

svc -> jwt : signAccessToken({ userId, email, role })
jwt --> svc : accessToken (15 min)
svc -> jwt : signRefreshToken({ userId, email })
jwt --> svc : refreshToken (7 days)

svc -> rtr : create(userId, refreshToken)
rtr -> hash : hash.token(refreshToken)
rtr -> db : refresh_tokens.create\n(status stored hashed, 7-day expiry)

svc --> ctrl : { pending: false, user, accessToken, refreshToken }
ctrl -> ctrl : set cookies\naccessToken (httpOnly, 15m)\nrefreshToken (httpOnly, 7d)

ctrl --> client : 201 "Registration successful"\n{ user: { id, email, status: "active",\nprofile, role: "Beneficiary" }, demand: null }

@enduml

============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/sequence_diag/sequence_registration_company.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - Company Registration Sequence (pending + demand + documents)
title Company Registration - Sequence Diagram (POST /v1/auth/register)

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam actor {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam participant {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}

actor "Company" as client

participant "RateLimit\n+ Upload\n+ zodValidate\n(middlewares)" as mid
participant "AuthController" as ctrl
participant "AuthService" as svc
participant "Hash\n(argon2)" as hash
participant "Prisma / DB" as db
participant "MinIO\nstorage" as minio

client -> mid : POST /v1/auth/register\n(email, password, first_name,\nlast_name, role_id, domain_ids?, files[1..5])
alt 429 rate limit exceeded (30 requests / hour per IP)
    mid --> client : 429 "Too many requests, please try again later"
end
alt 400 upload error (MIME not allowed / file over 10MB)
    mid --> client : 400 file error
end
alt 400 zod validation error
    mid --> client : 400 validation errors
end

mid -> ctrl : authController.register(req, files)
ctrl -> svc : register(data, files)

svc -> db : users.findUnique({ email })
alt email already exists
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 409
    ctrl --> client : 409 "Email already registered"
end

svc -> db : roles.findFirst({ id: role_id, is_selectable: true })
alt role missing or not selectable
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "Invalid role"
end

svc -> hash : hash.password(password)\n(argon2id)
hash --> svc : passwordHash

note over svc : domain_ids are OPTIONAL for a Company\n(no "at least one domain" gate)

alt files.length == 0
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "At least one document is required"
end

alt domain_ids provided
    svc -> db : domains.findMany({ id: { in: domain_ids } })
    alt returned count != domain_ids.length
        svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
        ctrl --> client : 400 "Some domains do not exist"
    end
end

svc -> db : $transaction\nusers.create(status=pending) + profiles.create\ndemands.create(status=pending)
note over svc : demand_domains.createMany\nonly if domain_ids were sent
db --> svc : { user, demandId }

loop each uploaded file (1..5)
    svc -> minio : storage.upload(BUCKETS.documents,\n"demands", file, userId)
    minio --> svc : objectName "demands/{userId}/{uuid}{ext}",\nfileSize, mimeType
    svc -> db : documents.create(type="demand", demand_id)
end

svc --> ctrl : { pending: true, user, demandId, documents }
note over ctrl : NO cookies set - no access/refresh token issued
ctrl --> client : 201 "Registration submitted for review"\n{ user, demand: { id } }

note over svc : On approval, demand_domains are copied to\nreviewer_domains only when role == "Reviewer"\n(demands.service.ts:46) - a Company keeps none

@enduml

alt 429 rate limit exceeded (30 requests / hour per IP)
    mid --> client : 429 "Too many requests, please try again later"
end
alt 400 upload error (MIME not allowed / file over 10MB)
    mid --> client : 400 file error
end
alt 400 zod validation error
    mid --> client : 400 validation errors
end

ctrl -> svc : register(data, files)

svc -> db : users.findUnique({ email })
alt email already exists
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 409
    ctrl --> client : 409 "Email already registered"
end

svc -> db : roles.findFirst({ id: role_id, is_selectable: true })
alt role missing or not selectable
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "Invalid role"
end

    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "Reviewer must select at least one domain"

    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "At least one document is required"

alt returned count != domain_ids.length
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "Some domains do not exist"
end

svc -> db : $transaction\nusers.create(status=pending) + profiles.create\ndemands.create(status=pending)\n+ demand_domains.createMany
db --> svc : { user, demandId }

loop each uploaded file (1..5)
    svc -> minio : storage.upload(BUCKETS.documents,\n"demands", file, userId)
    minio --> svc : objectName "demands/{userId}/{uuid}{ext}",\nfileSize, mimeType
    svc -> db : documents.create(type="demand", demand_id)
end

svc --> ctrl : { pending: true, user, demandId, documents }
note over ctrl : NO cookies set - no access/refresh token issued
ctrl --> client : 201 "Registration submitted for review"\n{ user, demand: { id } }

============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/1.puml
============================================================
@startuml
title Users & Auth
skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
hide empty members

' ========== ENUMS ==========

enum LoginResult {
    SUCCESS
    FAILED
}

enum UserStatus {
    active
    inactive
    locked
    pending
}

enum Gender {
    male
    female
}

' ========== USERS & AUTH ==========

class Users {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + email: VARCHAR (UNIQUE) {field}
    + password_hash: VARCHAR (bcrypt) {field}
    + status: VARCHAR (UserStatus) {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

class Profiles {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + user_id: INT (FK, UNIQUE) {field}
    + first_name: VARCHAR {field}
    + last_name: VARCHAR {field}
    + phone: VARCHAR {field}
    + date_of_birth: DATE {field}
    + gender: VARCHAR (Gender) {field}
    + avatar: VARCHAR {field}
    + bio: VARCHAR {field}
    + location: VARCHAR {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

class Roles {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + name: VARCHAR (UNIQUE) {field}
    + is_selectable: BOOLEAN {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

class UserRoles {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + user_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + role_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    ' @@unique (user_id, role_id)
}

class Permissions {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + name: VARCHAR (UNIQUE) {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

class RolePermissions {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + role_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + permission_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    ' @@unique (role_id, permission_id)
}

class RefreshTokens {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + user_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + token: VARCHAR (UNIQUE) {field}
    + expires_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

class LoginHistory {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + user_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + ip_address: VARCHAR {field}
    + user_agent: VARCHAR {field}
    + login_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + result: VARCHAR (LoginResult) {field}
    + failure_reason: VARCHAR (wrong_password, locked, not_verified...) {field}
}

class Devices {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + user_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + device_fingerprint: VARCHAR (UNIQUE) {field}
    + device_name: VARCHAR {field}
    + last_active: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

class UserSocials {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + user_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + platform_id: INT (FK) {field}
    + url: VARCHAR {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    ' @@unique (user_id, platform_id)
}

class Platforms {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + name: VARCHAR (UNIQUE) (linkedin, github, twitter, behance...) {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + updated_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

class Verifications {
    + id: INT (PK) {field}
    + user_id: INT (FK) {field}

    + type: VARCHAR (email_verify, password_reset) {field}
    + expires_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + used_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
    + created_at: TIMESTAMP {field}
}

' =========================================================

' Domain hierarchy
Domains "1" --> "0..*" SubDomains : contains

' Skill classification
SkillCategories "1" --> "0..*" Skills : categorizes

' Skills ↔ SubDomains
Skills "1" --> "0..*" SkillDomains : mapped through

' Users ↔ SubDomains

SubDomains "1" --> "0..*" UserSubDomains : joined

' Users ↔ Skills

' Users ↔ Languages

title JADARA - Projects & Review

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
hide empty members


' =========================================================
' ENUMS
' =========================================================

enum ProjectStatus {
    DRAFT
    SUBMITTED
    UNDER_REVIEW
    VERIFIED
    REJECTED
}

enum ProjectMemberRole {
    OWNER
    DEVELOPER
    DESIGNER
    CONTRIBUTOR
}

enum EvidenceType {
    GITHUB
    LIVE_DEMO
    FIGMA
    DOCUMENT
    SCREENSHOT
    VIDEO
    OTHER
}

enum DocumentType {
    CV
    CERTIFICATE
    PROJECT
    WORK_DOC
    DEMAND
    OTHER
}


' =========================================================
' PROJECTS
' =========================================================

class Projects {
    + id : UUID
    --
    status : ProjectStatus
    created_at : TIMESTAMP
    updated_at : TIMESTAMP
}


' =========================================================
' PROJECT INFORMATION
' =========================================================

class ProjectInfo {
    + id : UUID
    --
    project_id : UUID (UNIQUE)
    title : VARCHAR(200)
    description : TEXT
    subdomain_id : UUID
    requirements : JSON
    github_url : VARCHAR(500)
    live_url : VARCHAR(500)
    figma_url : VARCHAR(500)
    created_at : TIMESTAMP
    updated_at : TIMESTAMP
}


' =========================================================
' PROJECT EVIDENCE
' =========================================================

class Evidence {
    + id : UUID
    --
    project_id : UUID
    document_id : UUID (NULLABLE)
    type : EvidenceType
    title : VARCHAR(255)
    description : TEXT
    external_url : VARCHAR(500)
    created_at : TIMESTAMP
    updated_at : TIMESTAMP
}


' =========================================================
' PROJECT MEMBERS
' =========================================================

class ProjectMembers {
    + id : UUID
    --
    project_id : UUID
    user_id : UUID
    role : ProjectMemberRole
    joined_at : TIMESTAMP
    created_at : TIMESTAMP
    updated_at : TIMESTAMP
    ' @@unique (project_id, user_id)
}


' =========================================================
' PROJECT REVIEWS
' =========================================================

class ProjectReviews {
    + id : UUID
    --
    project_id : UUID
    reviewer_id : UUID
    rating : INT (1-10)
    feedback : TEXT
    created_at : TIMESTAMP
    updated_at : TIMESTAMP
    ' @@unique (project_id, reviewer_id)
}


' =========================================================
' CENTRAL DOCUMENTS TABLE
' =========================================================

class Documents {
    + id : UUID
    --
    user_id : UUID
    name : VARCHAR(255)
    original_name : VARCHAR(255)
    storage_path : VARCHAR(500)
    mime_type : VARCHAR(100)
    file_size : BIGINT
    document_type : DocumentType
    created_at : TIMESTAMP
    updated_at : TIMESTAMP
}


' =========================================================
' EXTERNAL REFERENCES
' =========================================================

class Users {
    + id : UUID
}

class SubDomains {
    + id : UUID
    --
    domain_id : UUID
    name : VARCHAR(100)
}

class Domains {
    + id : UUID
    --
    name : VARCHAR(100)
}


' =========================================================
' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

' Project ↔ Project Information
Projects "1" -- "1" ProjectInfo : has

' Project ↔ Evidence
Projects "1" -- "0..*" Evidence : has
Documents "0..1" -- "0..*" Evidence : file backing


' Project ↔ Users
Projects "1" -- "0..*" ProjectMembers : has members
Users "1" -- "0..*" ProjectMembers : participates


' Project ↔ Reviews
Projects "1" -- "0..*" ProjectReviews : receives
Users "1" -- "0..*" ProjectReviews : reviews


' Reviewer ↔ SubDomains
SubDomains "1" -- "0..*" ProjectInfo : classifies


' SubDomain ↔ Domain
Domains "1" -- "0..*" SubDomains : contains


' Documents belong to users
Users "1" -- "0..*" Documents : owns


' =========================================================
' NOTES
' =========================================================

' Project lifecycle: DRAFT -> SUBMITTED -> UNDER_REVIEW -> VERIFIED / REJECTED

' ProjectInfo: project's actual info. requirements stored as JSON.
' project_id UNIQUE (one project = one info).

' Evidence: replaces the old JSON evidence blob. type maps to
' Documents when EvidenceType = DOCUMENT, otherwise external_url.

' Documents: central storage metadata used by Certifications, Education,
' Work Experience, Projects/Evidence, Demands. storage_path is an internal key.

' ProjectMembers: one or many users per project (OWNER/DEVELOPER/DESIGNER/CONTRIBUTOR).
' Unique (project_id, user_id).

' ProjectReviews: a project can receive many reviews; a reviewer can review many.
' Reviewer must have Reviewer role + SubDomain matching ProjectInfo.subdomain_id.
' Unique (project_id, reviewer_id).

@enduml

' =========================================================
' RELATIONSHIPS
' =========================================================

' Course
CourseTypes "1" -- "0..*" Courses : type
SubDomains "1" -- "0..*" Courses : classifies

' Trainer
Users "1" -- "0..1" TrainerProfiles : profile
Courses "1" -- "0..*" CourseTrainers : has trainers
TrainerProfiles "1" -- "0..*" CourseTrainers : teaches

' Course structure
Courses "1" -- "0..*" CourseSections : contains
CourseSections "1" -- "0..*" Lessons : contains

' Lesson documents
Lessons "1" -- "0..*" LessonDocuments : resources
Documents "1" -- "0..*" LessonDocuments : attached documents

' External services / live lessons
Lessons "1" -- "0..*" LessonSchedules : schedules
ExternalServices "1" -- "0..*" LessonSchedules : provider
Documents "1" -- "0..*" LessonSchedules : recordings

' Enrollment
Courses "1" -- "0..*" CourseEnrollments : has students
Users "1" -- "0..*" CourseEnrollments : enrolls

' Progress
CourseEnrollments "1" -- "0..*" LessonProgress : tracks
Lessons "1" -- "0..*" LessonProgress : progress

' Course reviews
Courses "1" -- "0..*" CourseReviews : receives
Users "1" -- "0..*" CourseReviews : writes

' Lesson comments
Lessons "1" -- "0..*" LessonComments : has comments
Users "1" -- "0..*" LessonComments : writes

' Recursive comments / replies
LessonComments "0..1" --> "0..*" LessonComments : replies

' Assessments
Courses "1" -- "0..*" Assessments : has
Assessments "1" -- "0..*" AssessmentQuestions : contains
AssessmentQuestions "1" -- "0..*" AssessmentOptions : options
Assessments "1" -- "0..*" AssessmentAttempts : attempts
Users "1" -- "0..*" AssessmentAttempts : takes

' Certificates
Courses "1" -- "0..*" CourseCertificates : awards
Users "1" -- "0..*" CourseCertificates : receives
Documents "1" -- "0..*" CourseCertificates : certificate file


' =========================================================
' NOTES
' =========================================================

' Courses.requirements stored as JSON:
' { "skills": [{"skill_id": 12, "level": "INTERMEDIATE"}], "languages": ["English"], "other": [...] }

' LessonSchedules: only for lessons requiring a specific date/time (e.g. Friday 18:00-20:00, Google Meet).
' recording_document_id can point to the recording after the session.

' CourseEnrollments: student enrolls when access rules allow.
' Course completion can require required lessons/assignments completed + required assessment passed.
' Then: status = COMPLETED, completed_at = timestamp.

' CourseReviews: only enrolled students can review. UNIQUE(course_id, user_id).

' LessonComments: parent_comment_id nullable. NULL = top-level, non-null = reply (recursive threads).

' CourseCertificates: generated after successful completion. UNIQUE(course_id, user_id).

@enduml

============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/auth_all_class_diagram.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - Auth & Authorization: All Classes
title Auth & Authorization - All Classes

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam class {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam nodeFontSize 12
skinparam classAttributeFontSize 10
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0

' =========================================================
' ENUMS
' =========================================================

enum UserStatus {
    active
    inactive
    locked
    pending
}

' =========================================================
' CLASSES
' =========================================================

class users {
    + id : Int (PK)
    + email : String (UNIQUE)
    + password : String
    + status : UserStatus
    + role_id : Int (FK)
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + getAll(query: { page?, limit? }) : Object
    + getById(id: number) : Object
    + update(id: number, data: UpdateUserInput, adminUserId: number) : Object
    + remove(id: number, adminUserId: number) : void
    + getPublicProfile(id: number) : Object
    + getMyProfile(userId: number) : Object
    + updateMyProfile(userId: number, data: UpdateProfileInput, file?) : Object
    + getActivity(userId: number) : Object
    + addSocial(userId: number, platform: string, url: string) : Object
    + removeSocial(userId: number, socialId: number) : void
}

class profiles {
    + id : Int (PK)
    + user_id : Int (FK, UNIQUE)
    + first_name : String
    + last_name : String
    + phone : String
    + date_of_birth : DateTime
    + gender : String
    + avatar : String
    + bio : String
    + location : String
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..
    + updateAvatar(userId: number, avatarUrl: string) : Object
    + findProfileById(id: number) : Object
    + findPublicProfileById(id: number) : Object
}

note top of profiles
  profile fields (first_name, last_name, phone,
  date_of_birth, gender, bio, location, avatar)
  are updated via users.updateMyProfile()
  (PATCH /v1/users/me/profile)
end note

class roles {
    + id : Int (PK)
    + name : String (UNIQUE)
    + is_selectable : Boolean
    + created_at : DateTime
    + updated_at : DateTime
    ..

    + create(name: string) : Role
    + update(id: number, name: string) : Role
    + delete(id: number) : void
    + countUsersByRoleId(id: number) : number
    + getUsersByRoleId(roleId: number) : Object[]
    + getPermissionsByRoleId(roleId: number) : Permission[]
}

    + updated_at : DateTime

    + getRolesByPermissionId(permissionId: number) : Role[]
}

    + permission_id : Int (FK)

    ..

    + removePermissionFromRole(roleId: number, permissionId: number) : void
    + isPermissionAssignedToRole(roleId: number, permissionId: number) : boolean
    + findRolesByPermissionId(permissionId: number) : Role[]

    + id : Int (PK)
    + user_id : Int (FK)
    + token : String (UNIQUE)
    + expires_at : DateTime
    + created_at : DateTime
    ..
    + create(userId: number, rawToken: string, tx?) : RefreshToken
    + revokeAllByUserId(userId: number) : void
    + deleteExpired() : void

    + action : String
    + entity : String
    + entity_id : Int

    + new_values : Json

' RELATIONS
' =========================================================

roles "1" -- "0..*" users : has
users "1" -- "0..1" profiles : has
roles "1" -- "0..*" role_permissions
permissions "1" -- "0..*" role_permissions
users "1" -- "0..*" refresh_tokens : owns
users "1" -- "0..*" login_history : owns
users "1" -- "0..*" devices : uses
users "0..1" -- "0..*" audit_logs : triggers

users ..> UserStatus : uses

note top of roles
  is_selectable: true -> chosen at register,
  false -> hidden from public roles API (Admin)
end note

note top of role_permissions
  Unique (role_id, permission_id)
end note

note top of refresh_tokens
  token stored hashed (SHA-256),
  expires after 7 days
end note

note top of devices
  fingerprint = userId:userAgent:ip,
  device_name parsed from user-agent
end note

============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/exapmle.cv.txt
============================================================
JADARA - CV AUDIT & VERSION LOGIC

1. USER CHANGES PROFILE DATA

---

Every CV-relevant change creates one record in CvProfileAudits.

Examples:

User adds a skill:
Audit #11
component = SKILL
action = CREATED

User updates work experience:
Audit #12
component = WORK_EXPERIENCE
action = UPDATED

User adds a certification:
Audit #13
component = CERTIFICATION
action = CREATED

2. CURRENT CV

---

The current CV stores the ID of the last audit that was included
when the CV was generated.

Example:

```
CV Version = 5
last_audit_id = 10
is_current = true
```

This means:

```
CV Version 5 contains all CV-relevant changes
up to Audit #10.
```

## 3. USER REQUESTS A CV

When the user requests a CV:

```
latest_audit_id = 13
current_cv.last_audit_id = 10
```

The system checks:

```
latest_audit_id > current_cv.last_audit_id

13 > 10

TRUE
```

Therefore:

```
NEW CV IS REQUIRED.
```

## 4. MULTIPLE CHANGES

If the user made several changes:

```
Audit #11 → added React skill
Audit #12 → updated work experience
Audit #13 → added certification
```

The system does NOT generate:

```
CV Version 6
CV Version 7
CV Version 8
```

Instead, it generates ONLY ONE new CV:

```
CV Version 6
```

This CV contains the user's CURRENT data, including
all changes #11, #12 and #13.

5. AFTER GENERATING THE NEW CV

---

The new CV becomes:

```
version = 6
last_audit_id = 13
is_current = true
```

The previous CV becomes:

```
version = 5
is_current = false
```

## 6. USER REQUESTS THE CV AGAIN

Current CV:

```
version = 6
last_audit_id = 13
```

Latest audit:

```
latest_audit_id = 13
```

Check:

```
13 > 13

FALSE
```

Therefore:

```
NO NEW CV IS GENERATED.
```

The system returns the existing CV Version 6.

7. SIMPLE BUSINESS RULE

---

```
IF latest_audit_id > current_cv.last_audit_id
    THEN generate new CV
ELSE
    return current CV
```

## 8. IMPORTANT CONCEPT

CvProfileAudits = records WHAT CHANGED.

CvGenerationRequests = records WHICH CV VERSION WAS GENERATED.

last_audit_id = checkpoint telling the system
which changes are already included in the current CV.

## FINAL FLOW

User changes data
|
v
Create CvProfileAudit
|
v
User requests CV
|
v
Compare latest_audit_id
with current_cv.last_audit_id
|
+----------------------+
|                      |
v                      v
No new changes         New changes
|                      |
v                      v
Return current CV       Generate new CV
|
v
version + 1
|
v
last_audit_id = latest_audit_id
|
v
New current CV

' ========== NOTES ==========

note top of Users
  **User Status:**
  - active: normal account
  - inactive: disabled
  - locked: banned
  - pending: not verified yet
end note

note top of Roles
  **is_selectable:**
  - true  -> can be chosen at register
  - false -> hidden from public roles API
  Admin is always false
end note

note top of UserSkills
  **SkillLevel:**
  - beginner
  - intermediate
  - advanced
  - expert
end note

note top of UserLanguages
  **LanguageProficiency:**
  - native
  - fluent
  - advanced
  - intermediate
  - beginner
end note

note top of Projects
  **Status flow:**
  draft -> submitted -> under_review
  -> verified (first rating)
end note

note top of Evidence
  **Type:**
  - file  -> uploaded (file_url)
  - link  -> external (external_url)
end note

note top of ProjectReviews
  **Rating model:**
  - reviewer rates project from 1-10
  - project average = mean of all ratings
  - reviewer can update their rating
  - reviewer must be assigned to the project's
    domain via ReviewerDomains
  - unique (project_id, reviewer_id)
end note

note top of CvGenerationRequests
  **Status flow:**
  pending -> processing -> completed
  failed / incomplete
  (incomplete sets missing_fields)
end note

note top of Documents
  **Type:**
  - certificate -> cert file
  - project     -> project asset
  - workDoc     -> work document
  - demand      -> reviewer/company proof (linked to a Demands row)
end note

note top of Demands
  **Registration demand flow:**
  - Reviewer / Company register with proof documents
    and (reviewer) requested domains
  - user created with status = pending
  - admin approves -> user becomes active +
    reviewer_domains created from DemandDomains
  - admin rejects  -> user becomes inactive
  - unique (demand_id, domain_id) in DemandDomains
end note

@enduml

============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/sequence_login.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - Login Sequence (all users + pending case)
title Login - Sequence Diagram (POST /v1/auth/login)

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam actor {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam participant {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}

actor "User\n(Beneficiary / Reviewer /\nCompany / Admin)" as client

participant "RateLimit\n+ zodValidate\n(middlewares)" as mid
participant "AuthController" as ctrl
participant "AuthService" as svc
participant "Hash\n(argon2)" as hash
participant "Prisma / DB" as db
participant "RefreshTokenRepo" as rtr
participant "LoginHistoryRepo" as lhr
participant "DeviceRepo" as dev
participant "JWT\n(jwtUtils)" as jwt

client -> mid : POST /v1/auth/login\n(email, password)
alt rate limit / validation error
    mid --> client : 429 / 400
end

mid -> ctrl : authController.login\n(also reads ip + user-agent)
ctrl -> svc : login({ email, password }, ip, userAgent)

svc -> db : users.findUnique({ email })\ninclude profiles, roles, user_socials
alt user not found
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 401
    ctrl --> client : 401 "Invalid email or password"
end

alt status != "active"
    note over svc : pending = not accepted by admin yet\ninactive / locked = disabled
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 403
    ctrl --> client : 403 "Account is not active"\n(company/reviewer pending not accepted)
end

svc -> hash : verify(hashedPassword, password)
alt wrong password
    svc -> lhr : create(userId, ip, userAgent, success=false)
    lhr -> db : login_history.create
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 401
    ctrl --> client : 401 "Invalid email or password"
end

svc -> jwt : signAccessToken({ userId, email, role })
jwt --> svc : accessToken (15 min)
svc -> jwt : signRefreshToken({ userId, email })
jwt --> svc : refreshToken (7 days)

svc -> db : "prisma.$transaction"
    svc -> rtr : create(userId, refreshToken, tx)
    rtr -> hash : hash.token(refreshToken)
    svc -> lhr : create(userId, ip, userAgent, success=true, tx)
    svc -> dev : upsert(userId, ip, userAgent, tx)
    note over dev : "fingerprint = userId + userAgent + ip"

svc --> ctrl : { user, accessToken, refreshToken }
ctrl -> ctrl : set cookies\naccessToken (httpOnly, 15m)\nrefreshToken (httpOnly, 7d)

ctrl --> client : 200 "Login successful"\n{ user: { id, email, status, profile,\nrole, user_socials } }

@enduml

============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/sequence_registration.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - Registration Sequence Diagram
title Registration - Sequence Diagram (POST /v1/auth/register)

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam actor {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam participant {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}

actor "Client" as client

participant "RateLimit\n+ Upload\n+ zodValidate\n(middlewares)" as mid
participant "AuthController" as ctrl
participant "AuthService" as svc
participant "Hash\n(argon2)" as hash
participant "Prisma / DB" as db
participant "RefreshTokenRepo" as rtr
participant "MinIO\nstorage" as minio
participant "JWT\n(jwtUtils)" as jwt

client -> mid : POST /v1/auth/register\n(email, password, first_name,\nlast_name, role_id, domain_ids?, files)
alt 429 rate limit exceeded / 400 file error / 400 validation error
    mid --> client : 429/400\n(rate limit, upload, zod errors)
end

mid -> ctrl : authController.register\n(req, files)
ctrl -> svc : register(data, files)

svc -> db : users.findUnique({ email })
alt email exists
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 409
    ctrl --> client : 409 "Email already registered"
end
alt role not selectable
    svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
    ctrl --> client : 400 "Invalid role"
end

svc -> hash : hash.password(password)

alt role == "Beneficiary"
    svc -> db : $transaction - users.create\n(status=active) + profiles.create
    db --> svc : user
    svc -> jwt : signAccessToken + signRefreshToken
    jwt --> svc : tokens
    svc -> rtr : create(userId, refreshToken)\n(store hashed, 7 days)
    rtr -> db : refresh_tokens.create
    svc --> ctrl : { pending: false, user, accessToken, refreshToken }
    ctrl -> ctrl : set cookies\n(accessToken 15m, refreshToken 7d,\nhttpOnly)
    ctrl --> client : 201 "Registration successful"\n{ user, demand: null }
else role == "Reviewer" / "Company"
    alt no domain_ids (Reviewer)
        svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
        ctrl --> client : 400 "Reviewer must select at least one domain"
    end
    alt no files
        svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
        ctrl --> client : 400 "At least one document is required"
    end
    alt invalid domain_ids
        svc --> ctrl : AppError 400
        ctrl --> client : 400 "Some domains do not exist"
    end

    svc -> db : $transaction - users.create(status=pending)\n+ profiles.create + demands.create\n+ demand_domains.createMany
    db --> svc : { user, demandId }

    loop each uploaded file
        svc -> minio : storage.upload(files, BUCKETS.documents)
        minio --> svc : objectName, fileSize, mimeType
        svc -> db : documents.create\n(type="demand", demand_id)
    end

    svc --> ctrl : { pending: true, user, demandId, documents }
    note over ctrl : NO cookies set - user cannot login yet
    ctrl --> client : 201 "Registration submitted for review"\n{ user, demand: { id }, documents }
end

@enduml

============================================================
FILE: doc/diagrams/use_case_diagram.puml
============================================================
@startuml
' JADARA - Simple Use Case Diagram (include / extend)
title JADARA - Simple Use Case Diagram

skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
skinparam actor {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
skinparam usecase {
    BackgroundColor #F8F9FA
    BorderColor #333333
    FontName Helvetica
}
left to right direction

actor "Beneficiary" as b
actor "Reviewer" as r
actor "Company" as c
actor "Admin" as a

usecase "Login" as UC1
usecase "Verify credentials" as UC2
usecase "Register account" as UC3
usecase "Submit registration demand\n(upload proof docs + domains)" as UC12
usecase "Manage profile" as UC4
usecase "Manage CV data" as UC5
usecase "Generate CV PDF" as UC6
usecase "Upload documents" as UC7
usecase "Submit project" as UC8
usecase "Review project" as UC9
usecase "Approve / reject demand" as UC10
usecase "Manage users & roles" as UC11

b --> UC1
r --> UC1
c --> UC1
a --> UC1

b --> UC3
r --> UC12
c --> UC12

b --> UC4
b --> UC5
b --> UC6
b --> UC7
b --> UC8

r --> UC9
a --> UC10
a --> UC11

UC1 ..> UC2 : <<include>>
UC6 ..> UC5 : <<include>>
UC9 ..> UC8 : <<extend>>
UC12 ..> UC3 : <<include>>
UC10 ..> UC12 : <<extend>>

@enduml

============================================================
FILE: doc/domains/create-domain.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  CREATE DOMAIN API — POST /v1/domains
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Permission required: create_domain
================================================================

Setup — get an admin token first:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }' \
  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "name": "Robotics" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. WITHOUT PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Login as a user whose role lacks create_domain:

curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/user-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Robotics" }'

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Forbidden" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. VALIDATION ERRORS — Zod (422)
--------------------------------------------------

--- Missing name ---
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "description": "no name given" }'

Expected: 422
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Validation failed" }

--- Empty name ---
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "" }'

Expected: 422

--- Name longer than 100 chars ---
-- 101 characters:
LONG=$(python3 -c "print('a'*101)")
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d "{ \"name\": \"$LONG\" }"

Expected: 422

--- Description longer than 500 chars ---
LONGDESC=$(python3 -c "print('b'*501)")
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d "{ \"name\": \"Valid Name\", \"description\": \"$LONGDESC\" }"

Expected: 422

--- Empty body ---
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{}'

Expected: 422


--------------------------------------------------
4. DUPLICATE NAME (409)
--------------------------------------------------
-- "Web Development" already exists in seed:

curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Web Development", "description": "duplicate test" }'

Expected: 409
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Domain name already exists" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. SUCCESS — NAME ONLY (201)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Robotics" }'

Expected: 201
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Domain created successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 9,
    "name": "Robotics",
    "description": null,
    "created_at": "...",
    "updated_at": "..."
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
6. SUCCESS — NAME + DESCRIPTION (201)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Embedded Systems", "description": "Hardware and IoT development" }'

Expected: 201
Response: same shape as above with description filled

================================================================
  DELETE DOMAIN API — DELETE /v1/domains/:id

  Permission required: delete_domain

Setup — get an admin token first:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \

  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------
-- Login as a user whose role lacks delete_domain:

curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9 -b /tmp/user-cookies.txt

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Forbidden" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. INVALID ID — NOT A NUMBER (400)

Expected: 400
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid domain ID" }


--------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9999 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 404
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Domain not found" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. DOMAIN WITH LINKED SKILLS (409 — BLOCKED)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Seed links skills to domains via skill_domains:
--   SELECT d.name, COUNT(*) FROM skill_domains sd
--   JOIN domains d ON d.id = sd.domain_id GROUP BY d.name;

curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/1 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 409
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Cannot delete domain with linked skills" }
DB: nothing deleted


--------------------------------------------------
6. SUCCESS — DOMAIN WITHOUT SKILLS (200)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Create a fresh one first:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Temp Domain" }'
-- note the returned id (example: 10)

curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/10 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Domain deleted successfully", "data": null }


--------------------------------------------------
7. VERIFY DELETION (404 AFTER)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/10 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 404
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Domain not found" }

FILE: doc/domains/domain-reviewers.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  DOMAIN REVIEWERS APIS
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Admin only (permission: manage_users)
  Rate limit: 60 requests per minute
================================================================

Two admin-only endpoints to see which reviewers are assigned to
domains (via reviewer_domains, created on demand approval or
directly by admin).

  1. GET /v1/domains/reviewers        — all domains with reviewers
  2. GET /v1/domains/:id/reviewers    — one domain's reviewers


--------------------------------------------------
1. LIST ALL DOMAINS WITH THEIR REVIEWERS
--------------------------------------------------
GET /v1/domains/reviewers   (NOTE: route must be requested before
any /v1/domains/:id pattern — it is registered first)

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/reviewers" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Reviewers fetched successfully",
  "data": [
    {
      "id": 1,
      "name": "Web Development",
      "reviewers": [
        {
          "id": 5,
          "email": "reviewer@jadara.com",
          "status": "active",
          "profile": { "first_name": "Review", "last_name": "Er", "avatar": null },
          "assigned_at": "2026-08-28T16:00:00.000Z"
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": 2,
      "name": "Mobile Development",
      "reviewers": []
    },
    { "id": 8, "name": "Databases", "reviewers": [ { "id": 5, "email": "reviewer@jadara.com", "status": "active", "profile": {...}, "assigned_at": "..." } ] }
  ]
}

Domains without reviewers return an empty "reviewers" array.


--------------------------------------------------
2. GET ONE DOMAIN'S REVIEWERS
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/1/reviewers" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Reviewers fetched successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 1,
    "name": "Web Development",
    "reviewers": [
      {
        "id": 5,
        "email": "reviewer@jadara.com",
        "status": "active",
        "profile": { "first_name": "Review", "last_name": "Er", "avatar": null },
        "assigned_at": "2026-08-28T16:00:00.000Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
3. DOMAIN NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/999/reviewers"

Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Domain not found"}


--------------------------------------------------
4. INVALID ID (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/abc/reviewers"

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Invalid domain ID"}


--------------------------------------------------
5. NOT ADMIN (403) / NO AUTH (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/reviewers"   -> 403 Forbidden
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/reviewers"                                    -> 401
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/1/reviewers" -> 403 Forbidden

Note: regular authenticated users CAN list domains via GET /v1/domains
(view_domains) but NOT the reviewers endpoints (manage_users).


NOTES
- "reviewers" comes from reviewer_domains (users with role Reviewer
  explicitly assigned to the domain).
- Assigned when: demand approved (Reviewer role) or admin assigns via
  users/:id/review-domains.
- reviewer.profile contains first_name, last_name, avatar (no email
  inside profile).

============================================================
FILE: doc/domains/domain-skills.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  DOMAIN ↔ SKILLS APIs
    GET  /v1/domains/:id/skills   — list skills of a domain
    POST /v1/domains/:id/skills   — admin links a skill
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Permissions:
    GET  -> view_domains   (Admin + all baseline roles)
    POST -> update_domain  (Admin only)
================================================================

Setup:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }' \
  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

==================================================
GET /v1/domains/:id/skills
==================================================

--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/1/skills

Expected: 401 {"success":false,"message":"Unauthorized"}

--------------------------------------------------
2. INVALID ID (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/abc/skills -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Invalid domain ID"}

--------------------------------------------------
3. DOMAIN NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9999/skills -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Domain not found"}

--------------------------------------------------
4. SUCCESS (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/1/skills -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Skills fetched successfully",
  "data": [
    { "id": 1,  "name": "JavaScript",     "description": null, "status": "active" },
    { "id": 3,  "name": "HTML/CSS",       ... },
    { "id": 4,  "name": "React",          ... },
    ...
  ]
}

-- Domain with no linked skills returns data: []


==================================================
POST /v1/domains/:id/skills
=================================================
Body: EITHER single skill OR many skills:
  { "skill_id": <number> }
  { "skill_ids": [<number>, ...] }   (1–50 ids, duplicates removed)

Business logic (PARTIAL MODE — accepts what exists, reports the rest):
  1. validate shape            -> 422 if neither / both fields
  2. domain must exist         -> 404
  3. duplicates in input       -> auto-deduped
  4. each id lands in ONE bucket:
       added          = exists & newly linked      -> inserted (createMany)
       already_linked = exists & already attached  -> skipped, no error
       not_found      = does NOT exist             -> refused & REPORTED back
  5. always 200 on success (even if all ids were fake: added: [])
     only errors left: 404 bad domain / 403 no permission / 422 bad shape
  6. response includes full refreshed skill list

--------------------------------------------------
5. WITHOUT PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Only roles with update_domain (Admin):

curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/2/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/noperm-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "skill_id": 13 }'

Expected: 403 {"success":false,"message":"Forbidden"}

--------------------------------------------------
6. VALIDATION ERRORS (422)
--------------------------------------------------

--- Empty body ---
-d '{}'
Expected: 422 {"success":false,"message":"Validation failed",
               "errors":{"skill_id":["Either skill_id or skill_ids is required"]}}

--- Both fields at once ---
-d '{ "skill_id": 20, "skill_ids": [21] }'
Expected: 422 {"success":false,"message":"Validation failed",
               "errors":{"skill_id":["Provide either skill_id or skill_ids, not both"]}}

--- Empty array ---
-d '{ "skill_ids": [] }'
Expected: 422 errors.skill_ids ["Too small: expected array to have >=1 items"]

--- Bad values ---
-d '{ "skill_id": -1 }'              Expected: 422
-d '{ "skill_ids": [0, "x", null] }' Expected: 422

--------------------------------------------------
7. DOMAIN NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9999/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "skill_ids": [1, 2] }'

Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Domain not found"}

--------------------------------------------------
8. SUCCESS — SINGLE FORM (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "skill_id": 13 }'

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Skill assigned successfully",
  "data": {
    "added": [13],
    "already_linked": [],
    "not_found": [],
    "skills": [ ...full refreshed skill list of the domain... ]
  }
}

--------------------------------------------------
8b. SUCCESS — BULK FORM with MIXED results (200)
--------------------------------------------------
-- say 30/31 exist & unlinked, 9999 doesn't, duplicate 30 deduped:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/3/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "skill_ids": [30, 31, 9999, 30] }'

Expected: 200
{ "success": true, "message": "Skills assigned successfully",
  "data": { "added": [30, 31],
            "already_linked": [],
            "not_found": [9999],
            "skills": [...] } }

-- Replay same request -> added: [], already_linked: [30,31], not_found: [9999]
-- ALL ids fake        -> still 200: added: [], not_found: [...everything...]
-- Message says "Skill assigned..." when exactly 1 added, else "Skills assigned..."

DB: one row per NEW link in skill_domains (skill_id, domain_id)

--------------------------------------------------
10. VERIFY LINK PERSISTS (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/2/skills -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt | grep Python

Expected: Python present after re-fetch

FILE: doc/domains/list-domains.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  LIST DOMAINS API — GET /v1/domains
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Permission required: view_domains
================================================================

Setup — get an admin token first:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }' \
  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Auth works via cookie (accessToken) or header:
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>"

--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. EXPIRED TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Wait for access token to expire, then:

curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Access token expired" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. INVALID TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer garbage.token.here"

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid token" }


--------------------------------------------------
4. WITHOUT PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Login as Beneficiary user (has no view_domains):

curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/domains -b /tmp/user-cookies.txt

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Forbidden" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. DEFAULT PAGINATION (200)
--------------------------------------------------
-- No params: page=1, limit=10

curl -s http://localhost:3001/v1/domains -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Domains fetched successfully",
  "data": [
    {
      "id": 1,
      "name": "Web Development",
      "description": "Frontend and backend web technologies",
      "created_at": "...",
      "updated_at": "..."
    }
  ],
  "meta": {
    "total": 8,
    "page": 1,
    "limit": 10,
    "totalPages": 1,
    "nextCursor": null
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
6. CUSTOM PAGE & LIMIT (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains?page=2&limit=3" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Meta example:
{
  "total": 8,
  "page": 2,
  "limit": 3,
  "totalPages": 3,
  "nextCursor": 3
}
-- nextCursor = null when on last page


--------------------------------------------------
7. OUT-OF-RANGE PAGE (200, EMPTY DATA)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains?page=999" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, ..., "data": [], "meta": { "total": 8, "page": 999, ... } }


--------------------------------------------------
8. INVALID PAGE/LIMIT VALUES (200, FALLBACK TO DEFAULTS)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Non-numeric values fall back silently:
--   page=abc -> 1
--   limit=-5 -> 1
--   limit=500 -> capped at 100

curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/domains?page=abc&limit=500" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200 (no error, defaults applied)


--------------------------------------------------
9. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Send 61+ requests within 60 seconds:

for i in {1..61}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    http://localhost:3001/v1/domains -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt
done

Expected: first 60 return 200, 61st returns 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }
Headers: X-RateLimit-Limit: 60
         X-RateLimit-Remaining: 0
         Retry-After: <seconds>

Expected: 403

  -d '{ "name": "New Name" }'

Expected: 400
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid domain ID" }


--------------------------------------------------
4. VALIDATION ERRORS — Zod (422)
--------------------------------------------------

--- Empty name ---
curl -i -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "" }'

Expected: 422
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Validation failed" }

--- Name longer than 100 chars ---
LONG=$(python3 -c "print('a'*101)")
curl -i -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d "{ \"name\": \"$LONG\" }"

Expected: 422


--------------------------------------------------
5. DOMAIN NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9999 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \

  -d '{ "name": "Ghost Domain" }'
