6. DUPLICATE NAME (409)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Try renaming id=9 to a name owned by another row:

curl -i -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Web Development" }'

Expected: 409
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Domain name already exists" }

NOTE: renaming a domain to ITS OWN current name is allowed (no-op, no 409).


--------------------------------------------------
7. EMPTY BODY (200 — NO-OP UPDATE)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{}'

Expected: 200 (schema is all-optional; nothing changes)
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Domain updated successfully",
  "data": { "id": 9, "name": "...", ... }
}


--------------------------------------------------
8. SUCCESS — RENAME ONLY (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Robotics & Automation" }'

Expected: 200
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Domain updated successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 9,
    "name": "Robotics & Automation",
    "description": null,
    ...
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
9. SUCCESS — SET DESCRIPTION TO NULL (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/domains/9 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "description": null }'

Expected: 200
Response: data.description === null


============================================================
FILE: doc/important/auth-v2.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  AUTH MODULE — PLANNED FOR NEXT VERSION
  Priority: High / Medium / Low
================================================================


--------------------------------------------------
HIGH PRIORITY
--------------------------------------------------

1. REFRESH TOKEN REUSE DETECTION
   - If a stolen refresh token is used after rotation,
     revoke the entire token family for that user
   - Detects replay attacks


--------------------------------------------------
MEDIUM PRIORITY
--------------------------------------------------

2. ACCOUNT LOCKOUT AFTER N FAILED PASSWORDS
   - Lock account after 5 consecutive failed logins
   - Auto-unlock after 15 minutes or manual unlock
   - Add lock status to users table

3. EMAIL VERIFICATION ON REGISTER
   - Send verification email on registration
   - User must verify before account becomes active
   - Add email_verified field to users table

4. CSRF TOKENS
   - Add CSRF token to state-changing requests
   - Validate token on server side
   - SameSite=strict is not enough alone

5. CORS ORIGIN WHITELIST
   - Only allow requests from known frontend URLs
   - Block all other origins


--------------------------------------------------
LOW PRIORITY
--------------------------------------------------

6. PASSWORD HISTORY
   - Store last N password hashes per user
   - Prevent user from reusing old passwords

7. SESSION LIMIT PER USER
   - Max 3-5 active sessions per user
   - Force logout oldest session on new login

8. PHONE NUMBER VALIDATION
   - Validate format (e.g. +213XXXXXXXXX)
   - Reject invalid phone numbers

9. 2FA / MFA
   - TOTP authenticator (Google Authenticator)
   - Backup recovery codes


============================================================
FILE: doc/projects/admin-projects.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  ADMIN PROJECT APIS — GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /v1/projects/...
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Admin only (permission: manage_projects)
  Rate limits: read 60/min, write 20/min
================================================================

Admin variants of the project endpoints + status forcing.

--------------------------------------------------
1. LIST ALL PROJECTS — GET /v1/projects/all
--------------------------------------------------
Includes the project owner (users + profiles).

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/all?page=1&limit=10" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200 (data array + meta)
  item: id, user_id, ..., users: { id, email, profiles: {...} },
        rating: {average,count}|null, evidence_count

NOTE: /all must come BEFORE /:id in the router — it is defined first,
so GET /v1/projects/all won't be shadowed by /:id.


--------------------------------------------------
2. GET ONE PROJECT — GET /v1/projects/:id/admin
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/8/admin" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200 — same as owner detail + "users" object.
Includes: reviews[] (full list), rating {average,count}, evidence.
No ownership check (admin can view anyone).


--------------------------------------------------
3. GET ALL PROJECTS OF A USER — GET /v1/projects/user/:userId
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/user/10?page=1&limit=5"

Expected: 200 paginated — same shape as GET /v1/projects
(user's own list but triggered by admin for any user).


--------------------------------------------------
4. FORCE STATUS — PATCH /v1/projects/:id/status
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/8/status \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"status":"draft"}'

Expected: 200 {"success":true,"message":"Project status updated",
  "data":{"id":8,"status":"draft"}}
Status allowed: draft | submitted | under_review | verified.
status missing -> 400 "status is required".


--------------------------------------------------
5. DELETE ANY PROJECT — DELETE /v1/projects/:id/admin
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/7/admin

Expected: 200 "Project deleted successfully" (no status lock, unlike user delete).


--------------------------------------------------
6. ERRORS (shared)
--------------------------------------------------
not found          -> 404 Project not found
invalid id         -> 400 Invalid project ID
no auth            -> 401
non-admin cookie   -> 403 Forbidden (manage_projects required)

============================================================
FILE: doc/projects/create-project.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  CREATE PROJECT API — POST /v1/projects
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: any authenticated user (verifyAccessToken)
  Rate limit: 20 requests per minute
================================================================

Creates a new project in status "draft" for the logged-in user.

Body (JSON):
  title        string  required (1-255 chars)
  description  string  optional (max 2000)
  github_url   string  optional (must be a valid URL, max 500)
  live_url     string  optional (valid URL)
  figma_url    string  optional (valid URL)
  domain_id    int     optional (nullable) — project's domain

domain_id must exist in the domains table (see doc/domains/list-domains).
A project WITHOUT domain_id cannot be reviewed by reviewers later.


--------------------------------------------------
1. CREATE A PROJECT (201)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "title": "My Project",
    "description": "A showcase project",
    "github_url": "https://github.com/user/project",
    "live_url": "https://myproject.example.com",
    "domain_id": 1
  }'

Expected: 201
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Project created successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 9,
    "user_id": 10,
    "domain_id": 1,
    "title": "My Project",
    "description": "A showcase project",
    "github_url": "https://github.com/user/project",
    "live_url": "https://myproject.example.com",
    "figma_url": null,
    "status": "draft",
    "created_at": "...",
    "updated_at": "...",
    "domains": { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" },
    "evidence": [],
    "rating": null,                 // no reviews yet
    "reviews": [],                  // full review list (owner view)
    "evidence_count": 0
  }
}

DB: projects row inserted with status='draft'.


--------------------------------------------------
2. CREATE WITHOUT DOMAIN
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"title":"No Domain Project"}'

Expected: 201 — domain_id: null. Reviewer can never rate it
(rate endpoint returns 400 "Project has no domain assigned").


--------------------------------------------------
3. INVALID DOMAIN (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"title":"X","domain_id":999}'

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Domain not found: 999"}


--------------------------------------------------
4. VALIDATION (422)
--------------------------------------------------
Missing title:
  -d '{}'                                    -> 422 Validation failed
Bad URL: "github_url":"not-a-url"            -> 422
domain_id negative: "domain_id":-1           -> 422 (must be positive int)


--------------------------------------------------
5. NO AUTH (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects ...  -> 401


NOTES
- Project status flow: draft -> submitted -> under_review -> verified
  (see doc/projects/submit-project.txt).
- Domain list: GET /v1/domains (view_domains permission, any user).

============================================================
FILE: doc/projects/evidence.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  PROJECT EVIDENCE APIS
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: project owner only (verifyAccessToken)
  Rate limits: 10 requests per minute (evidence routes)
================================================================

Evidence = proof attachments on a project (files or links). Shown to
reviewers via GET /v1/reviews/:id (with download_url).

--------------------------------------------------
1. UPLOAD EVIDENCE FILE — POST /v1/projects/:id/evidence
--------------------------------------------------
multipart/form-data, field "file" (single), plus optional:
  type        (default "evidence")
  title       (default = original filename)
  description

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/8/evidence \
  -F "type=screenshot" \
  -F "title=Homepage" \
  -F "description=Front preview" \
  -F "file=@/path/to/screenshot.png"

Expected: 201 "Evidence uploaded successfully"
{
  "id": 2,
  "type": "screenshot",
  "title": "Homepage",
  "description": "Front preview",
  "file_url": "evidence/10/uuid.png",
  "external_url": null,
  "created_at": "..."
}
Stored in MinIO bucket "evidence" folder "evidence/<userId>/".


--------------------------------------------------
2. LINK A DOCUMENT — POST /v1/projects/:id/evidence/link
--------------------------------------------------
Links one of your own existing documents (see doc: document module)
as evidence without re-uploading.

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/8/evidence/link \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"document_id": 3}'

Expected: 201 "Evidence linked successfully"
  type "linked_document", title = document name,
  external_url "document:<id>". Requires the document to be YOURS
  (else 403), else 404 if document missing.


--------------------------------------------------
3. REMOVE EVIDENCE — DELETE /v1/projects/:id/evidence/:evidenceId
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/8/evidence/2

Expected: 200 "Evidence removed successfully"
  Deletes the MinIO file too (except linked documents).


--------------------------------------------------
4. ERRORS
--------------------------------------------------
No file attached            -> 400 "File is required"
Not owner of project        -> 403 Forbidden
Evidence not on this project-> 404 "Evidence not found"
Invalid project/evidence id -> 400
document_id missing         -> 400 "document_id is required"

============================================================
FILE: doc/projects/get-project.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  GET PROJECT API — GET /v1/projects/:id
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: project owner only (verifyAccessToken)
  Rate limit: 60 requests per minute
================================================================

Returns one of the logged-in user's projects with:
  - full reviews array (who rated, rating, feedback, dates)
  - rating summary { average, count }
  - evidence with download_url (presigned MinIO link, 1h)

Ownership is enforced: only user_id owner can view.


--------------------------------------------------
1. GET MY PROJECT (OWNER VIEW)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/8" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Project fetched successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 8,
    "user_id": 10,
    "domain_id": 1,
    "title": "Ben Project",
    "description": null,
    "github_url": null,
    "live_url": null,
    "figma_url": null,
    "status": "verified",
    "created_at": "...",
    "updated_at": "...",
    "domains": { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" },
    "rating": { "average": 8, "count": 1 },
    "reviews": [
      {
        "id": 5,
        "rating": 8,
        "feedback": "looks good",
        "created_at": "...",
        "updated_at": "...",
        "reviewer": {
          "id": 8,
          "email": "web.reviewer@test.com",
          "profiles": { "first_name": "Web", "last_name": "Reviewer", "avatar": null }
        }
      }
    ],
    "evidence": []
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
2. PROJECT NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/999"

Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Project not found"}

3. NOT OWNER (403)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/6"

Expected: 403 Forbidden (project belongs to user 6)


--------------------------------------------------
4. INVALID ID (400) / NO AUTH (401)
--------------------------------------------------
/projects/abc  -> 400 Invalid project ID
(no cookie)    -> 401


NOTES
- This is the answer to "get the review of the project by user":
  each review includes reviewer id/email/profile, rating 1-10,
  feedback, created_at/updated_at.
- Admin has a separate admin view: GET /v1/projects/:id/admin
  (see doc/projects/admin-projects.txt).

  LIST MY PROJECTS API — GET /v1/projects

Lists ONLY the logged-in user's projects (paginated). Each item

  GET /v1/projects/:id for that (see doc/projects/get-project.txt).

============================================================
FILE: doc/projects/submit-project.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  SUBMIT PROJECT API — POST /v1/projects/:id/submit
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: project owner only (verifyAccessToken)
  Rate limit: 20 requests per minute
================================================================

Submits a project for verification. Only draft projects can be
submitted. Status changes draft/submitted -> under_review.

NOTE: behavior of "submitted" status: the check allows both
draft and submitted to be re-submitted (both set under_review).

Flow:  draft -> under_review -> (reviews) -> verified


--------------------------------------------------
1. SUBMIT A DRAFT PROJECT
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/7/submit

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Project submitted for verification",
  "data": { "project_id": 7, "status": "under_review" }
}

DB: projects.status = 'under_review'


--------------------------------------------------
2. ALREADY UNDER REVIEW / VERIFIED (400)
--------------------------------------------------
Submitting a project already under_review or verified:

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/8/submit
(user project 8 is already verified)

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Only draft projects can be submitted"}


--------------------------------------------------
3. NOT OWNER (403)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/6/submit
(belongs to user 6)

Expected: 403 Forbidden


--------------------------------------------------
4. NOT FOUND (404) / INVALID ID (400) / NO AUTH (401)
--------------------------------------------------
/projects/999/submit  -> 404 Project not found
/projects/abc/submit  -> 400 Invalid project ID
(no cookie)           -> 401


NOTES
- Only after submission (under_review) can a reviewer rate it.
- After the FIRST rating, status becomes "verified" automatically.
- Admin can force any status via PATCH /v1/projects/:id/status
  (see doc/projects/admin-projects.txt).

============================================================
FILE: doc/projects/update-delete-project.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  UPDATE / DELETE PROJECT APIS
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: project owner only (verifyAccessToken)
  Rate limit: 20 requests per minute
================================================================

--------------------------------------------------
1. UPDATE — PATCH /v1/projects/:id
--------------------------------------------------
Fields (all optional):
  title, description, github_url, live_url, figma_url (nullable),
  domain_id (nullable, must exist)

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt \
  -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/7 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"title":"Updated Title","domain_id":8}'

Expected: 200 "Project updated successfully"
  (same response shape as getById — includes reviews/rating/evidence)

Errors:
  not owner            -> 403 Forbidden
  not found            -> 404 Project not found
  under review/verified-> 400 "Cannot edit a project that is under review or verified"
  bad url              -> 422
  bad domain_id        -> 400 "Domain not found: <id>"


--------------------------------------------------
2. DELETE — DELETE /v1/projects/:id
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/projects/7

Expected: 200 {"success":true,"message":"Project deleted successfully","data":null}

- Deletes evidence files from MinIO too (evidence bucket).
- Cannot delete a project under review or verified:
  -> 400 "Cannot delete a project that is under review or verified"

Errors: not owner 403, not found 404, invalid id 400.


NOTES
- Same ownership rules as GET /:id.
- Draft/submitted projects are editable/deletable; under_review and
  verified are locked (must use admin endpoints).

============================================================
FILE: doc/reviews/admin-reviewer-domains.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  ADMIN: ASSIGN REVIEWER DOMAINS — /v1/users/:id/review-domains
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Admin ONLY (permission: manage_users)
  Rate limits: read 60/min, write 20/min
================================================================

Admin manages which domains a reviewer is assigned to
(after these routes are under the admin-only router.use).

--------------------------------------------------
1. GET — GET /v1/users/:id/review-domains
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/users/18/review-domains" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{ "success": true, "message": "Reviewer domains retrieved",
  "data": [ { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" }, { "id": 8, "name": "Databases" } ] }


--------------------------------------------------
2. SET (REPLACE ALL) — PUT /v1/users/:id/review-domains
--------------------------------------------------
Replaces the reviewer's full domain set (deletes + recreates).

curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X PUT http://localhost:3001/v1/users/18/review-domains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"domain_ids":[1,8,3]}'

Expected: 200 "Reviewer domains updated" with the new domains array.

If a domain id does not exist:
Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Domains not found: 3"} (or list)


--------------------------------------------------
3. REMOVE ONE — DELETE /v1/users/:id/review-domains/:domainId
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/admin_cookies.txt \
  -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/users/18/review-domains/3

Expected: 200 "Reviewer domain removed" with the remaining domains.
Not assigned: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Reviewer domain assignment not found"}


--------------------------------------------------
4. ERRORS
--------------------------------------------------
invalid id        -> 400 "Invalid reviewer or domain ID"
no auth           -> 401
non-admin         -> 403 (manage_users)


NOTES
- These are the admin replacement for auto-assignment upon demand
  approval. Approving a reviewer creates reviewer_domains from the
  requested domains automatically (no manual step needed).

============================================================
FILE: doc/reviews/available-projects.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  AVAILABLE PROJECTS FOR REVIEW — GET /v1/reviews/available
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Reviewer ONLY (permission: review_projects)
  Rate limit: 60 requests per minute
================================================================

Shows projects the reviewer CAN rate:
  - project.status is under_review or verified
  - project.domain_id is in the reviewer's assigned domains
    (reviewer_domains table)
  - project is not the reviewer's own project
  - reviewer has NOT reviewed it yet

Reviewer must be assigned to the project's domain first
(GET /v1/reviews/domains shows your domains).


--------------------------------------------------
1. LIST AVAILABLE PROJECTS
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/available?page=1&limit=10" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Available projects fetched successfully",
  "data": [
    {
      "id": 8,
      "project_id": 8,
      "status": "verified",
      "submitted_at": "...",
      "rating": { "average": 8, "count": 1 },
      "project": {
        "id": 8,
        "title": "Ben Project",
        "description": null,
        "user": {
          "id": 10,
          "email": "ben@test.com",
          "profile": { "first_name": "Ben", "last_name": "User", "avatar": null }
        },
        "domain": { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" }
      }
    }
  ],
  "meta": { "total": 1, "page": 1, "limit": 10, "totalPages": 1, "nextCursor": null }
}


--------------------------------------------------
2. NO AUTH (401) / NON-REVIEWER (403)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/available"  -> 401
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/ben_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/available"  -> 403 Forbidden (review_projects)


NOTES
- A project already rated by you disappears from available
  (use /history for your previous ratings).
- A verified project stays available until you rate it.
- "submitted_at" = project.created_at (when the draft was created,
  not when submitted).

FILE: doc/reviews/project-detail.txt

================================================================
  PROJECT DETAIL FOR REVIEWER — GET /v1/reviews/:id

  Auth: Reviewer ONLY (permission: review_projects)

Full detail of ONE project for a reviewer:

Access allowed if: the project is in your assigned domains, OR you

        "feedback": "looks good",
        "created_at": "...",
        "reviewer": {
          "id": 8,
          "email": "web.reviewer@test.com",
          "profile": { "first_name": "Web", "last_name": "Reviewer", "avatar": null }
        }
      }
    ],
    "project": {
      "id": 8,
      "user_id": 10,
      "title": "Ben Project",
      "description": null,
      "status": "verified",
      "github_url": null,
      "live_url": null,
      "figma_url": null,
      "domain_id": 1,
      "created_at": "...",
      "updated_at": "...",
      "domains": { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" },
      "users": { "id": 10, "email": "ben@test.com", "profiles": { "first_name": "Ben", "last_name": "User", "avatar": null } },
      "evidence": [
        { "id": 1, "type": "screenshot", "title": "Home", "description": null, "file_url": "evidence/10/uuid.png", "external_url": null, "created_at": "...", "download_url": "http://localhost:9000/evidence/evidence/10/uuid.png?...presigned" }
      ]
    }
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
2. NO ACCESS (403)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt "http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/999"

Project not in your domains + not yours + not reviewed by you:
Expected: 403 {"success":false,"message":"This project is not in your assigned domains"}

Project id does not exist:
Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"Project not found"}


--------------------------------------------------
3. INVALID ID (400) / AUTH
--------------------------------------------------
/reviews/abc  -> 400 Invalid project ID
no cookie     -> 401
ben cookie    -> 403 (review_projects)


NOTES
- This is the reviewer's "project review" view for a project in a
  specific domain. Evidence file's download_url is a presigned MinIO
  link (valid 1 hour).
- The OWNER sees the same reviews via GET /v1/projects/:id
  (see doc/projects/get-project.txt); admin via /admin.

============================================================
FILE: doc/reviews/rate-project.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  RATE A PROJECT — POST /v1/reviews/:projectId/rate
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Reviewer ONLY (permission: review_projects)
  Rate limit: 10 requests per minute
================================================================

The reviewer rates a project that is in their assigned domain.
FIRST rating: creates the review and sets project.status -> "verified".

Body (JSON):
  rating     int   required, 1-10
  feedback   string optional, max 2000


--------------------------------------------------
1. RATE A PROJECT IN YOUR DOMAIN (201)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/8/rate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"rating":8,"feedback":"looks good"}'

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Rating submitted successfully",
  "data": {
    "project_id": 8,
    "rating": 8,
    "feedback": "looks good",
    "project_status": "verified",
    "rating_average": 8,
    "rating_count": 1
  }
}

DB: project_reviews row + projects.status = 'verified'
    (first rating only; count/avg recomputed)


--------------------------------------------------
2. ALREADY RATED BY THIS REVIEWER (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/8/rate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"rating":9}'

Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"You have already rated this project; use PUT /rate to update it"}


--------------------------------------------------
3. PROJECT NOT IN YOUR DOMAINS (403)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt \
  -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/999/rate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"rating":9}'

Expected: 403 {"success":false,"message":"This project is not in your assigned domains"}
(or 404 Project not found if id does not exist)


--------------------------------------------------
4. RATE YOUR OWN PROJECT (400)
--------------------------------------------------
Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"You cannot rate your own project"}


--------------------------------------------------
5. STATUS NOT REVIEWABLE (400)
--------------------------------------------------
Project not under_review/verified (e.g. draft):
Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Only projects under review can be rated"}


--------------------------------------------------
6. NO DOMAIN ASSIGNED (400)
--------------------------------------------------
project.domain_id is null:
Expected: 400 {"success":false,"message":"Project has no domain assigned; cannot be rated"}


--------------------------------------------------
7. VALIDATION (422) / AUTH
--------------------------------------------------
rating 0 or 11   -> 422
rating missing   -> 422
non-reviewer     -> 403 (review_projects)
no cookie        -> 401


NOTES
- Reviewer must be assigned to the project's domain (reviewer_domains)
  — set by admin on demand approval or via user review-domains API.
- Update a rating with PUT /v1/reviews/:projectId/rate.

============================================================
FILE: doc/reviews/reviewer-domains.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  REVIEWER DOMAINS APIs — GET/PUT/DELETE /v1/reviews/domains
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Reviewer ONLY (permission: review_projects)
  Rate limit: 60 requests per minute (read)
================================================================

The reviewer's assigned domains. Assigned when the reviewer's
registration demand is approved, or by admin via the user
review-domains API.

--------------------------------------------------
1. MY DOMAINS — GET /v1/reviews/domains
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt \
  "http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/domains" | python3 -m json.tool

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Reviewer domains fetched successfully",
  "data": [
    { "id": 1, "name": "Web Development" },
    { "id": 8, "name": "Databases" }
  ]
}


NOTES
- Only these domains' projects appear in GET /v1/reviews/available.
- After admin approves a reviewer demand, the requested domains are
  auto-created in reviewer_domains (see doc/demands/approve-demand.txt).
- Admin can override with the user review-domains endpoints:

============================================================
FILE: doc/reviews/update-rating.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  UPDATE RATING — PUT /v1/reviews/:projectId/rate
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: Reviewer ONLY (permission: review_projects)
  Rate limit: 10 requests per minute
================================================================

Updates the reviewer's OWN existing rating for a project
(does NOT change project_status).

Body (JSON):
  rating     int   required, 1-10
  feedback   string optional, max 2000


--------------------------------------------------
1. UPDATE MY RATING
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt \
  -X PUT http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/8/rate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"rating":9,"feedback":"even better after update"}'

Expected: 200
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Rating updated successfully",
  "data": {
    "project_id": 8,
    "rating": 9,
    "feedback": "even better after update",
    "project_status": "verified",
    "rating_average": 9,
    "rating_count": 1
  }
}

rating_average / rating_count are recomputed after the update.


--------------------------------------------------
2. NEVER RATED BEFORE (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -b /tmp/jadara/webrev_cookies.txt \
  -X PUT http://localhost:3001/v1/reviews/999/rate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"rating":7}'

If no review exists yet for you on that project:
Expected: 404 {"success":false,"message":"You have not rated this project yet"}


--------------------------------------------------
3. NOT IN YOUR DOMAINS (403)
--------------------------------------------------
Expected: 403 {"success":false,"message":"This project is not in your assigned domains"}


--------------------------------------------------
4. NO DOMAIN (400) / VALIDATION (422) / AUTH (401/403)
--------------------------------------------------
Same as POST /rate.


NOTES
- Unlike POST, PUT never flips project status.
- Use POST for the first rating, PUT for corrections.

============================================================
FILE: doc/skills/create-skill.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  CREATE SKILL API — POST /v1/skills
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Permission required: create_skill (Admin only)
================================================================

Setup:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }' \
  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "name": "Rust" }'

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. WITHOUT PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Beneficiary/Reviewer/Company have no create_skill:

curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/user-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Rust" }'

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Forbidden" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. VALIDATION ERRORS — Zod (422)
--------------------------------------------------

--- Missing name ---
-d '{ "description": "no name" }'
Expected: 422 {"success":false,"message":"Validation failed"}

--- Empty name ---
-d '{ "name": "" }'
Expected: 422

--- Name > 100 chars ---
LONG=$(python3 -c "print('a'*101)")
-d "{ \"name\": \"$LONG\" }"
Expected: 422

--- Invalid status value ---
-d '{ "name": "Rust", "status": "banana" }'
Expected: 422   (only "active" | "inactive" accepted)


--------------------------------------------------
4. DUPLICATE NAME (409)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "React", "description": "already seeded" }'

Expected: 409
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Skill name already exists" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. SUCCESS — MINIMAL (201)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Rust" }'

Expected: 201
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Skill created successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 36,
    "name": "Rust",
    "description": null,
    "status": "active",
    ...
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
6. SUCCESS — FULL BODY (201)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Vue.js", "description": "Progressive JS framework", "status": "active" }'

Expected: 201


--------------------------------------------------
7. SUCCESS — CREATED AS INACTIVE (201)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Legacy ASP", "status": "inactive" }'

Expected: 201, data.status === "inactive"


============================================================
FILE: doc/skills/delete-skill.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  DELETE SKILL API — DELETE /v1/skills/:id
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Permission required: delete_skill (Admin only)
================================================================

Setup:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }' \
  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401) / WITHOUT PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
Expected: 401 {"success":false,"message":"Unauthorized"}
          403 {"success":false,"message":"Forbidden"}
          (Beneficiary/Reviewer/Company have no delete_skill)


--------------------------------------------------
2. INVALID ID — NOT A NUMBER (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/abc -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 400
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid skill ID" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. SKILL NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/9999 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 404
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Skill not found" }


--------------------------------------------------
4. LINKED SKILL — BLOCKED (409)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Seeded skills are linked to domains via skill_domains,
-- e.g. JavaScript -> Web Development:

curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/1 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 409
Response:
{ "success": false, "message": "Cannot delete skill: linked to 1 domain(s). Unlink them first." }

-- Message lists ONLY the non-zero link types:
--   "...linked to 5 user(s). Unlink them first."
--   "...linked to 2 project(s), 1 domain(s). Unlink them first."

DB: nothing deleted


--------------------------------------------------
5. SUCCESS — UNLINKED SKILL (200)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Create a fresh one first:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Temp Skill" }'
-- note returned id (example: 37)

curl -i -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/37 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "Skill deleted successfully", "data": null }


--------------------------------------------------
6. VERIFY DELETION (404 AFTER)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/37 -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 404
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Skill not found" }

FILE: doc/skills/list-skills.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  LIST SKILLS API — GET /v1/skills
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Permission required: view_skills
  (granted to Admin + Beneficiary/Reviewer/Company)
================================================================

Setup:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }' \
  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/skills

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. WITHOUT PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Only reachable by a role without view_skills
-- (all seeded roles currently HAVE it):

curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/skills -b /tmp/noperm-cookies.txt

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Forbidden" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. DEFAULT PAGINATION (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s http://localhost:3001/v1/skills -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Skills fetched successfully",
  "data": [
    {
      "id": 1,
      "name": "JavaScript",
      "description": null,
      "status": "active",
      "created_at": "...",
      "updated_at": "..."
    }
  ],
  "meta": { "total": 35, "page": 1, "limit": 10, "totalPages": 4, "nextCursor": 2 }
}


--------------------------------------------------
4. CUSTOM PAGE & LIMIT (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/skills?page=2&limit=10" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Meta example: { "total": 35, "page": 2, "limit": 10, "totalPages": 4, "nextCursor": 3 }


--------------------------------------------------
5. LAST PAGE (200) — nextCursor null
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/skills?page=4&limit=10" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200, meta.nextCursor === null


--------------------------------------------------
6. OUT-OF-RANGE PAGE (200, EMPTY DATA)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/skills?page=99" -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200, data: []


--------------------------------------------------
7. INVALID PAGE/LIMIT (200, SILENT DEFAULTS)
--------------------------------------------------
page=abc -> 1 | limit=-5 -> 1 | limit=500 -> capped at 100

curl -s "http://localhost:3001/v1/skills?page=abc&limit=500" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200


--------------------------------------------------
8. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
-- skillRateLimit: 60 requests / 60 seconds per route

for i in {1..61}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    http://localhost:3001/v1/skills -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt
done

Expected: 60x 200 then 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }

FILE: doc/skills/update-skill.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  UPDATE SKILL API — PATCH /v1/skills/:id
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Permission required: update_skill (Admin only)
================================================================

Setup:

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }' \
  -c /tmp/admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
1. NO TOKEN (401) / WITHOUT PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
Same as create — verifyAccessToken then checkPermission("update_skill")

Expected: 401 {"success":false,"message":"Unauthorized"}
          403 {"success":false,"message":"Forbidden"}


--------------------------------------------------
2. INVALID ID — NOT A NUMBER (400)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/abc \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "X" }'

Expected: 400
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Invalid skill ID" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. VALIDATION ERRORS (422)
--------------------------------------------------

--- Invalid status ---
-d '{ "status": "archived" }'
Expected: 422   (only "active" | "inactive")

--- Name > 100 / empty name ---
Expected: 422 {"success":false,"message":"Validation failed"}


--------------------------------------------------
4. SKILL NOT FOUND (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -i -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/9999 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "Ghost" }'

Expected: 404
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Skill not found" }


--------------------------------------------------
5. DUPLICATE NAME (409)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Rename id=36 to an existing name:

curl -i -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/36 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "name": "React" }'

Expected: 409
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Skill name already exists" }

NOTE: renaming a skill to ITS OWN current name is a no-op success.


--------------------------------------------------
6. SUCCESS — TOGGLE STATUS (200)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -s -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/skills/36 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "status": "inactive" }'

Expected: 200
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Skill updated successfully",
  "data": {
    "id": 36,
    "name": "Rust",
    "description": null,
    "status": "inactive",
    ...
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
7. SUCCESS — SET DESCRIPTION TO NULL (200)
--------------------------------------------------
-d '{ "description": null }'
Expected: 200, data.description === null


--------------------------------------------------
8. EMPTY BODY (200 — NO-OP)
--------------------------------------------------
-d '{}'
Expected: 200, nothing changed

  DELETE USER API — DELETE /v1/users/:id

  -b beneficiary-cookies.txt

  -b admin-cookies.txt

  -b admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
for i in {1..11}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/users/2 \
    -b admin-cookies.txt

--------------------------------------------------
curl -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/users/2 \
  -b admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "message": "User deleted", "data": null }

DB: user + profile + refresh_tokens + login_history + devices all deleted (cascade)


--------------------------------------------------
8. VERIFY USER DELETED (404)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X GET http://localhost:3001/v1/users/2 \
  -b admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 404
Response: { "success": false, "message": "User not found" }

  LIST USERS API — GET /v1/users/
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Auth: manage_users permission required
================================================================


--------------------------------------------------
1. NO ACCESS TOKEN (401)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X GET http://localhost:3001/v1/users/

Expected: 401
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Unauthorized" }


--------------------------------------------------
2. WRONG PERMISSION (403)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Login as a user without manage_users permission:
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -c beneficiary-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "email": "user@jadara.com", "password": "Secret123" }'

curl -X GET http://localhost:3001/v1/users/ \
  -b beneficiary-cookies.txt

Expected: 403
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Forbidden" }


--------------------------------------------------
3. RATE LIMIT (429)
--------------------------------------------------
for i in {1..31}; do
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" \
    -X GET http://localhost:3001/v1/users/ \
    -b admin-cookies.txt
done

Expected: first 30 return 200, 31st returns 429
Response: { "success": false, "message": "Too many requests, please try again later" }


--------------------------------------------------
4. DEFAULT PAGINATION (page=1, limit=10)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X GET http://localhost:3001/v1/users/ \
  -b admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Users retrieved",
  "data": [ ... ],
  "meta": {
    "total": 3,
    "page": 1,
    "limit": 10,
    "totalPages": 1,
    "nextCursor": null
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------
5. CUSTOM PAGINATION (?page=1&limit=2)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X GET "http://localhost:3001/v1/users/?page=1&limit=2" \
  -b admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, ..., "meta": { "total": 3, "page": 1, "limit": 2, "totalPages": 2, "nextCursor": 2 } }


--------------------------------------------------
6. PAGE BEYOND TOTAL (?page=99)
--------------------------------------------------
curl -X GET "http://localhost:3001/v1/users/?page=99" \
  -b admin-cookies.txt

Expected: 200
Response: { "success": true, "data": [], "meta": { "total": 3, "page": 99, "limit": 10, "totalPages": 1, "nextCursor": null } }

============================================================
FILE: doc/users/user-skills.txt
============================================================
================================================================
  USER <-> SKILLS APIs
    Self-service (any logged-in user):
      GET    /v1/users/me/skills              my skills
      POST   /v1/users/me/skills              add 1..50 skills w/ levels
      PATCH  /v1/users/me/skills/:skillId     change one level
      DELETE /v1/users/me/skills/:skillId     remove one
    Admin (manage_users permission): same four under /v1/users/:id/...
  Base URL: http://localhost:3001
  Levels = DB enum SkillLevel (prisma/schema.prisma):
    beginner | intermediate | advanced | expert
  Validator imports SkillLevel from @prisma/client (single source of truth)
================================================================

Setup:

curl -s -c /tmp/u-cookies.txt -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{ "email": "admin@jadara.com", "password": "Admin@12345" }'

=================================================
GET /v1/users/me/skills
=================================================

-- no token -> 401 {"success":false,"message":"Unauthorized"}
curl -i http://localhost:3001/v1/users/me/skills

-- success (200), empty at first:
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Your skills fetched successfully",
  "data": []
}

Each entry shape:
{ "skill_id": 1, "name": "JavaScript", "level": "expert",
  "skill_status": "active", "added_at": "...Z" }

=================================================
POST /v1/users/me/skills   (bulk, per-skill levels)
=================================================
Body: { "skills": [ { "skill_id": <int>, "level": <enum> }, ... ] }
  - 1..50 items
  - duplicate skill_id in request -> deduped, FIRST level wins

Business logic (PARTIAL MODE), each id lands in ONE bucket:
    added          = exists & new      -> inserted
    already_linked = exists & had it   -> skipped, old level kept
    not_found      = doesn't exist     -> refused & reported back
Always 200 unless 401 / 404 user / 422 shape.

--- bad level (422) ---
-d '{ "skills": [ { "skill_id": 2, "level": "novice" } ] }'
Expected: 422 errors.skills ["Invalid option: expected one of ..."]

--- empty array / missing field (422) ---
-d '{ "skills": [] }'   and   -d '{}'

--- SUCCESS mixed (200) ---
curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3001/v1/users/me/skills \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -b /tmp/u-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "skills": [
        { "skill_id": 1,  "level": "expert" },
        { "skill_id": 13, "level": "beginner" },
        { "skill_id": 9999, "level": "advanced" },
        { "skill_id": 1,  "level": "intermediate" } ] }'

Expected: 200  (dup id=1 deduped; FIRST level "expert" kept)
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Skills assigned successfully",
  "data": {
    "added":          [ { "skill_id": 1, "level": "expert" },
                        { "skill_id": 13, "level": "beginner" } ],
    "already_linked": [],
    "not_found":      [9999],
    "skills": [ ...full refreshed list... ]
  }
}
Message = "Skill assigned successfully" when exactly 1 added.

-- replay same request -> added: [], already_linked has both ids

=================================================
PATCH /v1/users/me/skills/:skillId
=================================================

curl -s -X PATCH http://localhost:3001/v1/users/me/skills/1 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -b /tmp/u-cookies.txt \
  -d '{ "level": "intermediate" }'

Expected: 200 {"success":true,"message":"Skill level updated successfully","data":null}

Errors:
  bad level "-d '{ \"level\": \"godmode\" }'"   -> 422
  skill not linked to me (:skillId = 25)        -> 404 "Skill is not assigned to this user"
  non-numeric :skillId                          -> 400 "Invalid skill ID"

=================================================
DELETE /v1/users/me/skills/:skillId
=================================================

curl -s -w " [%{http_code}]" -X DELETE http://localhost:3001/v1/users/me/skills/13 \
  -b /tmp/u-cookies.txt

Expected: 200 {"success":true,"message":"Skill removed successfully","data":null}
Repeat same DELETE -> 404 "Skill is not assigned to this user"

=================================================
ADMIN variants (/v1/users/:id/skills...) — manage_users only
=================================================

curl -s http://localhost:3001/v1/users/3/skills -b /tmp/admin-cookies.txt
Expected: 200 "User skills fetched successfully" (Aziz's list)

POST/PATCH/DELETE bodies identical to /me versions.
Non-admin token on these -> 403 Forbidden (manage_users gate).

DB notes:
- table user_skills: unique(user_id, skill_id) + level enum SkillLevel
- migration: add_skill_level_enum

# Jadara Backend Architecture

## Overview

Jadara is a RESTful backend API built with Node.js, TypeScript, Express 5, and PostgreSQL. It implements JWT authentication with refresh token rotation, role-based access control (RBAC), and follows a layered architecture with clear separation of concerns.

---

## Tech Stack

| Category         | Technology                        |
| ---------------- | --------------------------------- |
| Runtime          | Node.js 18+                       |
| Language         | TypeScript 5.8                    |
| Framework        | Express 5.1                       |
| Database         | PostgreSQL 16                     |
| ORM              | Prisma 6.19                       |
| Authentication   | JWT (access + refresh tokens)     |
| Password Hashing | Argon2id                          |
| Validation       | Zod 4                             |
| API Docs         | Swagger / OpenAPI 3.0             |
| Logging          | Pino                              |
| Process Manager  | PM2                               |
| Rate Limiting    | rate-limiter-flexible (in-memory) |

---

## Project Structure

```
jadara/
├── framework/                    # Reusable infrastructure layer
│   ├── config/
│   │   ├── env.ts                # Environment variable validation (Zod)
│   │   ├── prisma.ts             # Prisma client singleton
│   │   ├── logger.ts             # Pino logger config
│   │   └── swagger.ts            # Swagger/OpenAPI setup
│   ├── middleware/
│   │   ├── asyncHandler.ts       # Wraps async route handlers
│   │   ├── requestId.ts          # X-Request-Id header (UUID)
│   │   ├── verifyAccessToken.ts  # JWT access token verification
│   │   ├── verifyRefreshToken.ts # JWT refresh token + DB verification
│   │   ├── checkPermission.ts    # RBAC permission check (cached)
│   │   ├── zodValidate.ts        # Request body validation
│   │   ├── zodValidateQuery.ts   # Query string validation
│   │   ├── rateLimiter.ts        # In-memory rate limiting with headers
│   │   ├── originCheck.ts        # CORS origin verification
│   │   ├── errorHandler.ts       # Global error handler
│   │   ├── notFound.ts           # 404 handler
│   │   ├── upload.ts             # Multer file upload config
│   │   └── uploadErrorHandler.ts # Upload error handling
│   ├── types/
│   │   └── express.d.ts          # Express Request type augmentation
│   └── utils/
│       ├── AppError.ts           # Custom error class
│       ├── response.ts           # Standardized JSON responses
│       ├── jwt.ts                # JWT sign/verify helpers
│       ├── hash.ts               # Argon2id hash/verify helpers
│       ├── cache.ts              # In-memory cache (no Redis)
│       ├── email.ts              # Nodemailer transport + templates
│       └── pagination.ts         # Offset & cursor pagination helpers
├── prisma/
│   ├── schema.prisma             # Database schema (8 models)
│   └── seed.ts                   # Default permissions, roles, admin user
├── src/
│   ├── index.ts                  # Server entry point
│   ├── app.ts                    # Express app setup & route mounting
│   └── modules/
│       ├── auth/                 # Authentication (implemented)
│       │   ├── auth.routes.ts
│       │   ├── auth.controller.ts
│       │   ├── auth.service.ts
│       │   ├── auth.validator.ts
│       │   ├── refresh-token.repository.ts
│       │   ├── role.repository.ts
│       │   └── login-history.repository.ts
│       ├── users/                # User management (stub)
│       │   ├── users.routes.ts
│       │   ├── users.controller.ts
│       │   ├── users.service.ts
│       │   ├── users.validator.ts
│       │   └── user.repository.ts
│       ├── roles/                # Roles & permissions (stub)
│       │   ├── roles.routes.ts
│       │   ├── roles.controller.ts
│       │   ├── roles.service.ts
│       │   └── roles.validator.ts
│       └── permissions/          # (handled inside roles module)
├── doc/                          # Documentation
├── logs/                         # PM2 log files
├── scripts/                      # Backup scripts
├── tests/                        # Test files
├── ecosystem.config.cjs          # PM2 config
├── package.json
├── tsconfig.json
├── tsconfig.build.json
├── .env.example
├── .gitignore
└── README.md
```

---

## Architecture Patterns

### Layered Module Architecture

Each module follows a 4-layer pattern:

```
Routes → Controller → Service → Repository
  ↓         ↓          ↓          ↓
Middleware  Request/   Business   Prisma DB
chain      Response   logic      queries
```

- **Routes** — Define endpoints, apply middleware chain (auth, validation, rate limiting, permissions)
- **Controller** — Extract request data, call service, send response. Wrapped in `asyncHandler`
- **Service** — Business logic, throws `AppError` on failures
- **Repository** — Direct Prisma queries (data access only)

### Request Flow

```
Client Request
    ↓
requestId (X-Request-Id header)
    ↓
rateLimiter (in-memory, X-RateLimit-* headers)
    ↓
originCheck (CSRF protection for state-changing methods)
    ↓
zodValidate (request body / query validation)
    ↓
verifyAccessToken (JWT from cookie or Authorization header)
    ↓
checkPermission (RBAC from DB, cached 60s in memory)
    ↓
Controller → Service → Repository → Prisma → PostgreSQL
    ↓
response.success() / response.error()
    ↓
errorHandler (catches AppError and unhandled errors)
```

---

## Authentication

### JWT Token Strategy

| Token         | Expiry     | Payload                   | Storage                                |
| ------------- | ---------- | ------------------------- | -------------------------------------- |
| Access Token  | 15 minutes | `{ userId, email, role }` | httpOnly cookie + response body        |
| Refresh Token | 7 days     | `{ userId, email }`       | httpOnly cookie + DB (argon2id hashed) |

### Flows

**Registration:**

1. Validate input (Zod) — includes `role_id`
2. Check email uniqueness
3. Validate role exists and is one of: Beneficiary, Reviewer, Company
4. Hash password (argon2id)
5. Create user (status: `active`) + profile with chosen role
6. Sign access token (15min) + refresh token (7d)
7. Hash refresh token, store in DB
8. Return tokens

**Login:**

1. Validate input (Zod)
2. Find user by email
3. Check status = `active`
4. Verify password (argon2id)
5. Sign access token (15min) + refresh token (7d)
6. Hash refresh token, store in DB
7. Record login history (IP, user agent, success/fail)
8. Set httpOnly cookies

**Token Refresh:**

1. Verify refresh token JWT signature
2. Revoke all old refresh tokens for user
3. Sign new access token + refresh token
4. Hash new refresh token, store in DB

**Change Password:**

1. Verify old password (argon2id)
2. Hash new password
3. Update password in DB
4. Revoke all refresh tokens (force re-login on all devices)

**Logout:**

1. Revoke all refresh tokens for user
2. Clear refresh cookie

---

## Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)

### Database Models

```
users.role_id → roles.id (one user = one role)
roles ←→ role_permissions ←→ permissions
```

### Permission Check Flow

```
checkPermission("manage_users")
    ↓
Cache lookup: permissions:user:{userId}
    ↓ (miss)
Query: users → roles → role_permissions → permissions
    ↓
Cache result (60s TTL)
    ↓
Check if permission name exists in user's permissions
    ↓
Allow / Deny (403)
```

### Roles

| Role        | Description        | Selectable at Registration |
| ----------- | ------------------ | -------------------------- |
| Beneficiary | End user           | Yes                        |
| Reviewer    | Content reviewer   | Yes                        |
| Company     | Business account   | Yes                        |
| Admin       | Full system access | No (admin only)            |

---

## Rate Limiting

In-memory rate limiting using `rate-limiter-flexible`. Applied per endpoint with response headers:

| Header                  | Description              |
| ----------------------- | ------------------------ |
| `X-RateLimit-Limit`     | Max requests allowed     |
| `X-RateLimit-Remaining` | Requests remaining       |
| `X-RateLimit-Reset`     | Seconds until reset      |
| `Retry-After`           | Seconds to wait (on 429) |

### Rate Limits by Endpoint

| Endpoint        | Limit       |
| --------------- | ----------- |
| Login           | 10 req/min  |
| Register        | 3 req/hour  |
| Change Password | 5 req/5min  |
| Refresh Token   | 20 req/min  |
| Logout          | 20 req/min  |
| General         | 100 req/min |

---

## Database Schema (8 Models)

| Model              | Purpose                                                  |
| ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `users`            | User accounts (email, password hash, status, role_id)    |
| `profiles`         | User profile data (name, phone, avatar)                  |
| `roles`            | Role definitions (Beneficiary, Reviewer, Company, Admin) |
| `permissions`      | Permission definitions                                   |
| `role_permissions` | Role ↔ Permission junction                               |
| `refresh_tokens`   | Hashed refresh tokens                                    |
| `login_history`    | Login audit log                                          |
| `devices`          | Registered device fingerprints                           |

---

## What's Implemented vs What's Stubbed

### Implemented (working code)

- All framework middleware (13 files)
- All framework utilities (7 files)
- All configuration (4 files)
- Express type augmentation
- Prisma schema + seed
- All route definitions
- All Zod validators
- App setup and route mounting

### Stubbed (return 501, TODO comments)

| Module    | Files Missing Logic                                                                |
| --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **users** | `users.service.ts`, `users.controller.ts`, `user.repository.ts`                    |
| **roles** | `roles.service.ts`, `roles.controller.ts`, `roles.repository.ts` (new file needed) |

### Not Yet Executed

- `npm install`
- `npx prisma generate`
- `npx prisma migrate dev`
- `npx prisma db seed`

---

## Key Differences from Unv-Pro Reference

| Feature                | Unv-Pro                      | Jadara                             |
| ---------------------- | ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| Redis                  | Required (caching, sessions) | Not used (in-memory cache)         |
| MinIO                  | Required (file storage)      | Not used                           |
| Modules                | 15+ modules                  | 3 modules (auth, users, roles)     |
| User Roles             | Many-to-many (user_roles)    | One-to-many (users.role_id)        |
| Registration           | Email verification required  | No email verification              |
| Access Token Expiry    | 60 minutes                   | 15 minutes                         |
| Refresh Token Rotation | No                           | Yes (old token revoked on refresh) |
| Rate Limit Headers     | No                           | Yes (`X-RateLimit-*`)              |
| Request ID             | No                           | Yes (`X-Request-Id`)               |
| Health Check           | Simple OK                    | Verifies DB connectivity           |
| Audit Logging          | In-memory only               | `audit_logs` model (not yet used)  |
| Logger                 | `console.error` in places    | Pino everywhere                    |

## API Endpoints

### Auth (`/v1/auth`)

| Method | Endpoint           | Auth    | Description                    |
| ------ | ------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------ |
| POST   | `/register`        | No      | Register new user              |
| POST   | `/login`           | No      | Login                          |
| POST   | `/refresh-token`   | Refresh | Rotate refresh token           |
| POST   | `/change-password` | Access  | Change password (requires old) |
| POST   | `/logout`          | Access  | Logout                         |

### Users (`/v1/users`)

| Method | Endpoint      | Auth         | Description        |
| ------ | ------------- | ------------ | ------------------ |
| GET    | `/me/profile` | Access       | Get own profile    |
| PATCH  | `/me/profile` | Access       | Update own profile |
| GET    | `/`           | manage_users | List all users     |
| GET    | `/:id`        | manage_users | Get user by ID     |
| PATCH  | `/:id`        | manage_users | Update user        |
| DELETE | `/:id`        | manage_users | Delete user        |
| GET    | `/:id/role`   | manage_users | Get user's role    |

### Roles (`/v1/roles`)

| Method | Endpoint                         | Auth         | Description                               |
| ------ | -------------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/`                              | **No**       | List all roles (public, for registration) |
| GET    | `/:id`                           | manage_roles | Get role by ID                            |
| POST   | `/`                              | manage_roles | Create role                               |
| PATCH  | `/:id`                           | manage_roles | Update role                               |
| DELETE | `/:id`                           | manage_roles | Delete role                               |
| GET    | `/:id/permissions`               | manage_roles | Get role permissions                      |
| POST   | `/:id/permissions`               | manage_roles | Add permission to role                    |
| DELETE | `/:id/permissions/:permissionId` | manage_roles | Remove permission                         |
| GET    | `/:id/users`                     | manage_roles | Get users with role                       |

### Permissions (`/v1/permissions`)

| Method | Endpoint     | Auth         | Description               |
| ------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------------------- |
| GET    | `/`          | manage_roles | List all permissions      |
| GET    | `/:id`       | manage_roles | Get permission by ID      |
| POST   | `/`          | manage_roles | Create permission         |
| PATCH  | `/:id`       | manage_roles | Update permission         |
| DELETE | `/:id`       | manage_roles | Delete permission         |
| GET    | `/:id/roles` | manage_roles | Get roles with permission |

### Health

| Method | Endpoint  | Auth | Description                    |
| ------ | --------- | ---- | ------------------------------ |
| GET    | `/health` | No   | Health check (DB connectivity) |

FILE: framework/config/env.ts

import { z } from "zod";
import dotenv from "dotenv";

dotenv.config();

const envSchema = z.object({
  NODE_ENV: z.enum(["development", "production", "test"]).default("development"),
  PORT: z.coerce.number().default(3000),
  API_URL: z.string().default("http://localhost:3000"),
  CORS_ORIGINS: z.string().default("*"),
  SWAGGER_ENABLED: z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .transform((v) => {
      if (v !== undefined) return v === "true" || v === "1";
      return process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production";
    }),

  DATABASE_URL: z.string().url(),

  ACCESS_SECRET: z.string().min(16),
  REFRESH_SECRET: z.string().min(16),
  ACCESS_EXPIRY: z.string().default("15m"),
  REFRESH_EXPIRY: z.string().default("7d"),

  UPLOAD_DIR: z.string().default("uploads"),
  UPLOAD_MAX_FILE_SIZE: z.coerce.number().default(10 * 1024 * 1024),
  UPLOAD_MAX_AVATAR_SIZE: z.coerce.number().default(2 * 1024 * 1024),

  MINIO_ENDPOINT: z.string().default("localhost"),
  MINIO_PORT: z.coerce.number().default(9000),
  MINIO_ACCESS_KEY: z.string().default("minioadmin"),
  MINIO_SECRET_KEY: z.string().default("minioadmin"),
  MINIO_BUCKET: z.string().default("jadara"),
  MINIO_USE_SSL: z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .transform((v) => {
      if (v !== undefined) return v === "true" || v === "1";
      return false;
    }),

  FRONTEND_URL: z
    .string()
    .url()
    .optional()
    .transform((v) => (v === "" ? undefined : v)),

  // Local Postfix relay (Postfix handles the upstream SMTP auth)
  SMTP_HOST: z.string().default("127.0.0.1"),
  SMTP_PORT: z.coerce.number().int().default(25),
  SMTP_SECURE: z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .transform((v) => v === "true" || v === "1"),
  SMTP_FROM: z.string().default("Jadara <benallouaziz1414@gmail.com>"),
  SMTP_USER: z.string().optional(),
  SMTP_PASS: z.string().optional(),
});

const parsed = envSchema.safeParse(process.env);

if (!parsed.success) {
  console.error("❌ Invalid environment variables:");
  console.error(parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors);
  process.exit(1);
}

export const env = parsed.data;

FILE: framework/middleware/asyncHandler.ts

import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";

export const asyncHandler = (
  fn: (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => Promise<void>,
) => {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    fn(req, res, next).catch(next);
  };
};


============================================================
FILE: framework/middleware/checkPermission.ts
============================================================
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { cache } from "../utils/cache.js";
import prisma from "../config/prisma.js";
import { response } from "../utils/response.js";
import logger from "../config/logger.js";

export const checkPermission = (permissionName: string) => {
  return async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      if (!req.user?.userId) {
        response.error(res, "Unauthorized", 401);
        return;
      }

      const userId = req.user.userId;
      const cacheKey = `permissions:user:${userId}`;

      let permissions = await cache.get<string[]>(cacheKey);

      if (!permissions) {
        const user = await prisma.users.findUnique({
          where: { id: userId },
          include: {
            roles: {
              include: {
                role_permissions: {
                  include: { permissions: true },
                },
              },
            },
          },
        });

        if (!user || !user.roles) {
          permissions = [];
        } else {
          permissions = user.roles.role_permissions.map((rp) => rp.permissions.name);
        }

        await cache.set(cacheKey, permissions, 60);
      }

      if (!permissions.includes(permissionName)) {
        response.error(res, "Forbidden", 403);
        return;
      }

      next();
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error(
        { err, userId: req.user?.userId, permission: permissionName },
        "Permission check failed",
      );
      response.error(res, "Permission check failed", 500);
    }
  };
};


============================================================
FILE: framework/middleware/errorHandler.ts
============================================================
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { AppError } from "../utils/AppError.js";
import { response } from "../utils/response.js";
import logger from "../config/logger.js";

interface BodyParserError extends Error {
  status?: number;
  statusCode?: number;
  type?: string;
  expose?: boolean;
}

export const errorHandler = (
  err: Error,
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
  _next: NextFunction,
): void => {
  if (err instanceof AppError) {
    logger.error(
      {
        err,
        path: req.path,
        method: req.method,
        statusCode: err.statusCode,
        requestId: req.requestId,
      },
      "Application error",
    );
    response.error(res, err.message, err.statusCode);
    return;
  }

  const bodyErr = err as BodyParserError;
  if (bodyErr.type === "entity.too.large") {
    logger.warn(
      { path: req.path, method: req.method, requestId: req.requestId },
      "Request body too large",
    );
    response.error(res, "Request body too large", 413);
    return;
  }

  if (
    bodyErr.expose &&
    bodyErr.statusCode &&
    bodyErr.statusCode >= 400 &&
    bodyErr.statusCode < 500
  ) {
    logger.warn(
      { err, path: req.path, method: req.method, requestId: req.requestId },
      "Client request error",
    );
    response.error(res, err.message, bodyErr.statusCode);
    return;
  }

  if (err instanceof SyntaxError && "body" in err) {
    logger.error(
      { err, path: req.path, method: req.method, requestId: req.requestId },
      "Invalid JSON",
    );
    response.error(res, "Invalid JSON in request body", 400);
    return;
  }

  logger.error(
    { err, path: req.path, method: req.method, requestId: req.requestId },
    "Unhandled error",
  );
  response.error(res, "Internal server error", 500);
};

FILE: framework/middleware/originCheck.ts
============================================================
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { env } from "../config/env.js";

const STATE_CHANGING_METHODS = ["POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE"];

function allowedOrigins(): string[] {
  if (env.CORS_ORIGINS === "*") return ["*"];
  return env.CORS_ORIGINS.split(",")
    .map((s) => s.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function isSameOrigin(origin: string, host: string | undefined): boolean {
  if (!host) return false;
  try {
    return new URL(origin).host === host;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export const originCheck = (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
  if (!STATE_CHANGING_METHODS.includes(req.method)) {
    next();
    return;
  }

  const origin = req.headers.origin;
  const referer = req.headers.referer;

  if (!origin && !referer) {
    next();
    return;
  }

  const origins = allowedOrigins();
  const source = (origin ?? referer) as string;

  const allowed =
    origins.includes("*") ||
    origins.some((o) => {
      try {
        return o === new URL(source).origin;
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    }) ||
    isSameOrigin(source, req.headers.host);

  if (!allowed) {
    res.status(403).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Origin not allowed",
    });
    return;
  }

  next();
};
